
Chapter 1 Thriller Game, Professional Challenge


[You have been selected by the horror game to become one of the challengers in this round.]

[This round's challenge profession is taxi driver, with an overall danger level of B.]

[Every night, in this beautiful and terrifying world, many taxi drivers work diligently, providing great convenience to many eerie beings who don't have a hearse or don't want to drive one. They are an indispensable part of this horror world.]

[Taxi drivers work hard, but their income is relatively high. However, they occasionally encounter unreasonable customers, leading to some unpleasant situations.]

[You are the first batch of newly hired drivers. Tonight you will be making your first trip. What kind of strange events you will encounter, which route you will take, and where your destination will be all up to luck.]

[Please try your best to survive!]

[This professional challenge lasts for seven days. Challengers who are still alive after seven days will receive rewards commensurate with their performance.]

[The challenge officially begins.]

The cold, emotionless notification sounded, and Lin Bei's vision returned to normal; it was no longer pitch black.

His face was pale.

"Damn it, I got chosen..."

"And a taxi driver, a B-level danger level one."

"Seven days."

"Surviving will be incredibly difficult; taxi drivers will come into contact with many supernatural beings."

This horror game, which suddenly appeared years ago, pulls a group of people into a horror world every day, starting a game called a career challenge.

Once you become a challenger, you receive various job titles and must complete the corresponding tasks. Any non-cooperation will result in immediate elimination.

Completing the challenge alive will earn you corresponding rewards; the better your performance, the better the reward.

Each round of career challenges lasts varying, from three days to thirty days. The longest known challenge is to serve as the butler for a certain extremely powerful supernatural being for three years.

Death could happen at any moment during the challenge.

Failure to complete the job could lead to death, displeasing a customer could lead to death, even walking down the street could result in being killed by a ghost—it was extremely dangerous.

"Sigh~"

Lin Bei sighed and turned to look around.

He was currently in a parking lot, filled with hearses of the same model and color.

Besides him, there were eleven other people, all challengers participating in this round of the professional challenge.

Nine of them looked uneasy, fearfully looking around, constantly complaining.

"This is the worst luck I've ever had, running into this mess."

"Taxi drivers, you must encounter a lot of ghosts while working, one wrong move and you'll die..."

"Quit, I want to quit!"

"..."

Clearly, they, like Lin Bei, were newly selected challengers.

The remaining two were relatively calm, already observing the situation, clearly having relevant experience.

...

Three minutes later, a figure approached from not far away.

It was hideous, bloated, covered in swollen, dark red pustules, and had three eyes—yellow-green-red in color—always with one eye open and the other two closed.

"Hello everyone, I'm the vehicle supervisor."

"Are you the new employees who joined today?"

"Come and get your car keys."

"Choose your own. There are many cars, some in good condition, some in bad. Don't be smug if you pick a good one, and don't complain if you pick a bad one."

"Let me tell you in advance, each person's daily turnover must not be less than three hundred Terror Coins. If it's less than that, we'll make up for it with your blood and flesh or your lives."

"One by one, starting with you."

The vehicle manager randomly pointed to someone, gesturing for that person to come and get the keys.

At the same time, he pulled out a dozen or so keys from his pocket and casually threw them on the ground in front of him.

Judging from their appearance, these keys were all old; some were patina-covered, and others were rusty.

Although the person was extremely resistant, he dared not disobey orders. With a look of fear, he walked forward and randomly picked up a car key from the ground.

He pressed the unlock button, and the headlights of a taxi in the distance lit up.

A badly damaged taxi...

No windshield wipers, a third of the windshield shattered, only one rearview mirror, the left front tire completely worn out, and the car covered in scratches and dents. In the real world, this car wouldn't be allowed on the road without extensive repairs.

“Huh?”

“I have to earn 300 Terror Coins driving such a beat-up car?”

“How could that be possible!”

“What kind of monster would ride in such a dilapidated taxi…”

He was desperate.

The others also had mixed feelings.

They were all worried that they would end up driving such a broken-down car.

“Next.”

“You come.”

Another person was forced to choose a car.

Lin Bei stood at the back, frowning as he looked at the cars.

There were hundreds of cars, but two-thirds of them were broken-down.

And there were only a dozen or so keys to choose from. He already had an answer in his mind.

"Most of these keys correspond to severely damaged vehicles, just in varying degrees of damage. Even if you're lucky, you'll end up with a broken car. Reaching a turnover of 300 is really difficult."

"Headache!"

He was momentarily at a loss for what to do.

He'd already encountered a problem right from the start.

Suddenly, a cold, mechanical voice rang in Lin Bei's mind:

[Ding!]

[God-Level Favorability System Officially Activated!]

[The main function of this system is to increase the favorability points of the target. The increase rate is one point per second. It can only be used on one monster at a time. There is no upper limit to favorability, and it can be stopped at any time.]

[Once favorability is increased, it will not decrease.]

[System Level: lv1..]

[Affected Area: Wandering Soul level and below (Necromancer - Wandering Soul - Vengeful Spirit - Evil Spirit - Monster King - Monster Emperor - Disaster Level - Nightmare Level - Hell Level - Monster God.)]

[Experience Points: 0/1000 (One favorability point corresponds to one experience point)]

[Other functions will be unlocked when the system reaches a certain level!]

[The Lucky Roulette function will be unlocked after reaching lv2!]

"A system?!"

"I actually have a system!"

"And a system that can directly increase favorability points!"

"Incredible!"

Lin Bei was overjoyed.

He knew very well what a system was, having read many novels.

Suppressing his excitement, Lin Bei looked at the vehicle manager.

An information panel appeared before him.

[Traffic Light]

[Favorability Points: -10]

[Relationship: Treats you as a plaything]

[Level: Wandering Soul]

[...]

Originally a 300-pound fat man, he was hit by a taxi while running a red light one day, unfortunately crashing into the traffic light and dying.

Car destroyed, man dead, traffic light collapsed, driver fled.

After death, he merged with the traffic light, becoming this bizarre creature.

The scene he saw before he died was a taxi, so he came here to become a traffic controller, in order to find the taxi driver who hit him.

A rule-based bizarre creature!

When in its presence, you must obey traffic rules; once you run a red light, you will become its prey.

】

"-10 favorability points?"

"This guy is definitely up to no good!"

Without a second's hesitation, Lin Bei decisively decided to increase the bizarre creature's favorability.

The level just met the requirements.

"Increase favorability."

"Target: Traffic light."

【Ding!】

【Target locked!】

【Favorability has begun to increase!】



Chapter 2 Offering cigarettes and tea, brother, have a cigarette?


+1..

+1..

+1..

Only Lin Bei could see that a +1 appeared above the vehicle manager's head every second.

Ten seconds later, he checked the information panel again.

【Traffic Light】

【Affection Points: 0】

【Relationship: Just a passerby】

【..】

“It really is one point per second.”

“It’s already down to 0.”

..

The vehicle manager was completely unaware of the anomaly.

It was urging the second challenger.

“Hurry up and choose.”

“No need to hesitate.”

“The cars are all in good condition.”

After some deliberation, the second person finally chose a key based on intuition.

But the result wasn’t good; it was another severely damaged car, this time with only one door.

He stood dumbfounded in front of the car, his expression as if he’d eaten shit.

“What a piece of junk.”

“It’s worse than a toy car.”

A short while later, the third person also finished choosing.

Similarly, one broken-down car.

Then came the fourth person.

Broken-down car.

The fifth person.

Broken-down car.

The sixth person, the seventh person...

All broken-down cars!

The eighth person was one of the two non-newcomer challengers.

Seeing it was his turn, Li Hao walked forward. Just before reaching the vehicle manager, he took out a box of expensive cigarettes he had prepared beforehand.

“Manager, these are for you.”

“Necromancer's World brand cigarettes.”

Seeing this, the vehicle manager raised an eyebrow, seemingly satisfied with Li Hao's performance.

He reached out and took the cigarettes.

He tore open the packaging and pulled one out.

Li Hao, with keen insight, took out a lighter and lit the cigarette.

The vehicle manager took a satisfying drag, his mood visibly improving.

“Not bad, tastes great, I like it.”

Li Hao said with a smile:

“Then what do you think…”

He didn't finish his sentence, but the vehicle manager already knew what he meant.

The vehicle manager pulled another car key from his pocket and casually tossed it to Li Hao.

“Here.”

“This is a newly repaired car.”

“Enough for seven days.”

“Do a good job.”

Li Hao took the key and quickly pressed the unlock button. A taxi in the distance turned on its lights.

The exterior was intact, with no obvious damage, and very clean.

“Not bad.”

“Wasn’t a waste of my cigarette.”

“With this car, a little better service, and avoiding conflicts with customers, this challenge will be a breeze.”

He pressed the ignition button.

Vroom!

The engine roared to life, the dashboard lights up, and all he had to do was shift gears and accelerate.

The other challengers were stunned by this maneuver!

"Huh? You can do that? Trade a pack of cigarettes for a nice car?"

"Damn it, he's already so far ahead. If I were a monster, I'd definitely choose his car over mine, this one without even windows."

"Why didn't I think of that? If I'd known, I wouldn't have chosen this piece of junk!"

"What's the use of knowing? Do you have any Terror Coins to buy it?"

"Envious!"

"..."

Lin Bei was no exception.

He hadn't expected this kind of thing to exist; it was a closely guarded secret.

Only another challenger, not a newbie, remained calm.

He proactively said to the vehicle manager:

"Manager, can I choose a car first?"

"I've also prepared a small gift for you."

Hearing this, the vehicle manager became very interested.

"Okay."

"You choose first."

Yue Xiaoliang immediately walked towards the vehicle manager, taking out the gift he had prepared beforehand.

A large box of beautifully packaged tea.

"This is Styx Blood Tea, prepared for you."

"It has a rich aroma and can also refresh you; it's perfect for you."

The vehicle manager was very satisfied with this gift.

The tea was very practical.

He would drink it often.

"Here's a key for you."

"A car with only minor problems."

"That'll be enough for you."

Yue Xiaoliang took the key and left, receiving a perfectly good taxi in return.

Someone was unhappy.

"It's unfair! Why do they get to drive nice cars while we get to drive beat-up ones? This is clearly harassment!"

"I want a nice car too! You can't treat me like this just because I didn't give gifts!"

"It has to be fair! Everyone has to be treated equally, or we'll report you!"

"..."

They were protesting.

They all wanted a better car.

But it was useless; it only angered the vehicle manager.

"Shut up, all of you."

"One more argument and you'll be fined three thousand and kept here to work until you die!"

Instantly, everyone fell silent.

No one wanted to bear the consequences.

The challenge lasted seven days, requiring three hundred Terror Coins per day, totaling less than three thousand.

One of the strict requirements for completing the challenge was that the earnings couldn't be negative; even a single-digit amount would be considered completion. But if the earnings were negative, there would be serious consequences.

Yue Xiaoliang walked past the crowd, a disdainful look on his face.

"You think you can get preferential treatment without paying anything?"

"Dream on!"

"This is a horror game!"

"A bunch of idiots, just wait to die."

Yue Xiaoliang was hoping these people would pick a lousy car.

The more people did this, the fewer competitors there would be, the more Terror Coins he could earn, and the less danger he would face.

The other challengers didn't bother to curse Yue Xiaoliang.

They were in a panic...

Soon, the car selection process would end, and then the challenge would officially begin.

...

The vehicle manager, a cigarette dangling from his lips, placed the premium tea aside and looked at the remaining three.

"Next."

His gaze inadvertently swept over Lin Bei, then settled on him.

"Strange."

"Why does this guy look so pleasing to the eye?"

"I have an urge to sit down and have a drink with him, and have a good chat about the strange creatures."

"I don't understand."

"Let him choose."

Lin Bei was also looking at the vehicle manager.

However, he was looking at the top of his head...

+1...

+1...

The numbers kept appearing.

[Traffic Light]

[Affection Points: 55]

[Relationship: Want to Drink with You]

[...]

"Increased affection by 65 points."

"Soon to reach one hundred."

"Want to drink with me?"

"With affection at this level, he shouldn't deliberately make things difficult for me."

"But to be on the safe side, it's better to reach one hundred."

...

At this moment, the vehicle manager waved to Lin Bei.

"Buddy, why don't you choose first?"

A smile appeared on its ferocious face, making it look even more terrifying.

At this moment, everyone's attention was focused on Lin Bei.

Some were secretly cursing Lin Bei for choosing the worst car.

But no one expected Lin Bei to shake his head:

"I'll choose last."

"Let the others go first."

Everyone's expression changed; they thought Lin Bei was courting death.

The vehicle manager had just been enraged!

But now Lin Bei had done this, his attitude and tone completely impolite, even taking the lead.

"This guy's asking for it!"

"He dared to disobey that weird order; he's practically asking for death."

"What an idiot. He didn't offer cigarettes or tea; how could he expect preferential treatment?"

Actually, Lin Bei was also testing the waters.

He wanted to see how the vehicle manager would react to this request.

Another ten seconds passed, and his favorability points rose to 70.

A whole new stage.

[Traffic Light]

[Affection Points: 70]

[Relationship: Friend]

[It treats you as a friend and will help you as much as possible as long as it doesn't cross its bottom line.]

Just then, the vehicle manager reacted.

"Okay."

"Then you choose last."

"By the way, want a cigarette?"

"You've never tried a cigarette from Thriller World, have you?"

As it spoke, it walked towards Lin Bei, holding a cigarette in its hand.

Lin Bei naturally reached out and took the cigarette.

He put the cigarette in his mouth and was about to ask for a light.

But before he could even speak, the vehicle manager proactively took out a lighter and smiled as it lit the cigarette for Lin Bei.

"Have this for now."

"Just tell me if you need more."

"I'll go get busy."

With that, the vehicle manager turned and left.

Lin Bei took a deep drag of the cigarette.

A metallic taste of blood filled his mouth.

The smoke in the horror world is different from that in the ordinary world; it's not addictive, contains no harmful substances, and is more like a supplement, relieving stress while enhancing one's inner supernatural power.

"It tastes good."



Chapter 3: Affection surpasses 100, giving my brother the best car.


Everyone was stunned.

The situation hadn't unfolded as expected.

The vehicle manager not only didn't reprimand Lin Bei, but instead lit a cigarette for him, acting as if they were old friends. The two, one human and one ghost, stood together seamlessly.

"What's going on? It gave me a cigarette?!"

"I'm dumbfounded. Giving me a cigarette is one thing, but lighting it for me? Are you two having an affair?"

"Really? This ghost is so good-natured?!"

"It's like it's turned into a different ghost..."

"..."

The situation was very strange.

The vehicle manager's behavior was very odd; "outrageous" wouldn't be an exaggeration.

At this moment, someone mustered up the courage to ask:

"Manager..."

"I also want a cigarette, can I have one?"

Hearing this, the vehicle manager glared at the person, frowning impatiently.

It casually took out a key and smashed it forcefully on the person's head.

"You want a cigarette?"

"Are you worthy?"

"What right do you have to compare yourself to my brother?"

"Your task today is five hundred!"

"If you don't earn enough, I'll chop off two of your fingers!"

"You damned bastard!"

"Deadly World" brand cigarettes are expensive, usually costing three figures.

How could he just share such cigarettes with someone?

Especially this clown.

His status is lower than his, and he's under his control, yet he dares to ask for a cigarette?

What an idiot!

At this moment, everyone felt even more strange.

The contrast between the two, the difference in their treatment, was like heaven and earth.

The man was filled with regret.

"I'm such an idiot."

"Why did you ask such a question?"

"Now you've ruined yourself!"

...

Li Hao sat in the car, staring intently at Lin Bei, his hands gripping the steering wheel tightly, his eyes filled with malice.

He really wanted to get out of the car and snatch the cigarette from Lin Bei's mouth.

“Those are cigarettes I bought, a gift for that weirdo. I wouldn't even smoke them myself!”

“What right do you have to smoke cigarettes I bought!”

“This pack costs 180 Terror Coins, and there are only 20 cigarettes in it. That works out to 9 Terror Coins per cigarette.”

“Damn it!”

At the same time, Li Hao was also puzzled.

He didn't understand why the vehicle manager would offer Lin Bei cigarettes.

He was certain that Lin Bei was a newbie.

Because after becoming a formal challenger, one could view other people's information.

Information related to Lin Bei had appeared in Li Hao's view.

【Lin Bei】

【Level: Newbie】

【Completed Class: None】

【Overall Rating: None】

“A newbie, yet enjoying this kind of treatment?”

“He has a secret!”

“Could he be being kept by some powerful weirdo?”

“His looks certainly meet that requirement.”

Weirdos are similar to humans, possessing different personalities and hobbies.

Some monsters love to eat and spend their time researching food.

Some monsters love to kill and will try every means to increase their strength.

Naturally, there are also monsters that enjoy manipulating humans. Some monsters like to keep humans as pets, playing a game called a "human nurturing project," investing resources to cultivate a human so they can do other, more important things.

This is the only possibility Li Hao can think of right now.

"It should be him."

"Don't provoke him yet."

"We'll see how things develop later."

...

The vehicle supervisor returned to his original position and continued assigning vehicles.

“Next, come and choose a car.”

One person stepped forward and successfully picked a broken-down car.

At this point, ten people had finished choosing.

The result was eight cars in terrible condition and two relatively normal ones.

Only two people remained.

Soon, the eleventh person finished choosing. No gifts, no preferential treatment, and the result was naturally unsatisfactory: a beat-up car with a faulty engine.

At this moment, all the challengers were watching Lin Bei.

Only Lin Bei hadn't chosen yet.

They were very curious about what car Lin Bei would choose.

“Judging from his relationship with that monster, he definitely won't choose a broken-down car.”

“Most likely a normal taxi, like those two experienced challengers.”

“To be honest, I'm envious!”

“…”

The vehicle supervisor was also looking at Lin Bei.

The more it looked at Lin Bei, the more pleasing he seemed.

Before, it only wanted to drink and chat with Lin Bei. Now, it wanted to stay by Lin Bei's side forever, to help him solve his problems, and to do everything he wanted to do.

It knew this wasn't love.

It was a very special feeling!

A feeling comparable to a life-or-death bond.

The vehicle manager didn't know why it felt this way about Lin Bei.

It didn't want to know.

It just wanted to follow its heart and help Lin Bei.

"I want to help him in any way I can!"

"He's a taxi driver, and I'm in vehicle management."

"We can start with that."

"He's just become a Challenger, he's unfamiliar with this world, doesn't know many things, and doesn't have any Terror Coins, so even eating is a problem."

"There's so much we can do."

"But first, we need to get him driving a taxi; that's what Challengers have to do. I can do other things while he rests."

...

[Traffic Light]

[Affection Points: 136]

[Relationship: Life-or-Death Bond]

[It has come to regard you as a life-or-death friend and will spare no effort to help you. Just ask, and it will do it. Compared to your bond, money is insignificant.]

Lin Bei quietly looked at the information panel.

That line of description truly shocked him!

"Huh?"

"We've become sworn brothers?"

"That's incredible!"

"Now I don't have to worry about failing anymore."

"With this, I'm sure I can complete this challenge."

Without waiting for the vehicle manager's summons, Lin Bei walked forward.

His gaze swept over the keys on the ground.

There were only three keys left, each one worn and tattered. How could the corresponding vehicles be in good condition?

The vehicle manager had been watching Lin Bei and naturally noticed where his gaze was directed.

Immediately, it had a bold idea.

"The boss is away these days; it seems she went to the Moon King's territory."

"If I remember correctly, her car is parked in the garage."

"Arrange this car for her; some customers prefer luxury taxis."

Having made the decision, the vehicle manager put away the three keys.

"Wait a moment."

"These cars aren't suitable for you; they all have various problems."

"I specially prepared a car for you."

"I'll go drive it over to you now."

With that, he turned and left without hesitation.

Lin Bei could only wait.

A few minutes later, a hearse approached from afar.

It was a luxurious and prestigious-looking hearse. Although it had taxi-specific lights on the roof and the words "Abyss Taxi" and "Hail on Call" painted on its body, it couldn't hide its high-class status.

The vehicle manager got out of the car, handed Lin Bei a bunch of keys, and pointed to the vehicle, saying,

"You drive this car!"

"This car is 'Go Die, Shadow,' it costs millions, it's the most expensive taxi in the company."

"Don't doubt it, you heard right, it's a taxi, the kind that can pick up passengers, it's just that the fare is quite high. Others can only earn three hundred Terror Coins a day, but you can earn three hundred in one trip."

"How about it, satisfied?"

"Just drive this car however you like. I'll handle any problems, I'm very familiar with the insurance company."



Chapter 4: Humiliating yourself to drive a supercar—isn't that just pure simp behavior?


Lin Bei stared at the super-luxury car, *All Die, Ghost Shadow*, priced at millions of Terror Coins, and was momentarily at a loss for words to answer the vehicle manager's question.

"Satisfied?"

This...

Are you kidding me?!

This isn't about being satisfied or not!

I'm just an ordinary taxi driver, and you want me to drive a car worth millions to run a taxi?

Would any supernatural being actually take this kind of taxi?

No...

Would any supernatural being dare to take it?

"That's outrageous!"

"A little over a hundred favorability points and it's already like this."

"What if it were higher?"

The vehicle manager's information panel reappeared.

[Traffic Light]

[Favorability Points: 488]

[Relationship: Willing to Go Through Fire and Water for You]

[In its heart, you have become the most important person, even more important than its own life. It is willing to do anything for you, even go through fire and water, even fight against countless monsters, it will not hesitate for a moment, not even frown!]

"Holy crap!?"

"Five hundred favorability points?"

"It will reach one thousand in a few minutes."

"Now, even if I asked to switch to a train as a taxi, it would do everything in its power to fulfill my request."

"After all, compared to before, it has gained almost four hundred more favorability points."

...

Seeing that Lin Bei did not answer for a long time, the vehicle manager's heart sank, subconsciously taking this as a sign of dissatisfaction.

It couldn't help but frown, sensing that the situation was not good, and even its tone of voice was anxious.

"I understand, are you dissatisfied?"

"Brother, how about this? Give me some face, and let you drive this car for a few days. I'll get you a better one in the meantime!"

"Three days, three days at most, give me some time."

"If you don't like a multi-million dollar sedan, then I'll get you a supercar worth tens of millions!"

The vehicle manager had already begun planning what to do next.

Even though the horror world is vast and full of powerful and bizarre creatures, cars of this caliber are rare. Getting one wouldn't be easy, but no matter the difficulty, he was determined to do it, simply to meet Lin Bei's needs.

Even knowing the dangers involved!

Lin Bei finally came to his senses.

He quickly explained.

"I'm not dissatisfied, you misunderstood."

"This car is enough."

"Please don't do anything like that."

He meant it sincerely.

Using a multi-million dollar car as a taxi was already outrageous.

What if they brought in a multi-million dollar sports car as a taxi?

The absurdity would rise several levels!

But to the vehicle manager, Lin Bei was making a forced explanation, a display of kindness.

He stepped forward and patted Lin Bei's shoulder forcefully.

"It's okay, just say you want it."

"No need to hide anything from your brothers."

"You're my best brother, how could I not give you what you want?"

"Don't worry, don't try to save face for me."

Lin Bei experienced for the first time the feeling of being unable to explain himself.

He genuinely didn't want it, so how did it become a way to save face?

He wanted to say something more.

But when he saw the driver's exceptionally determined gaze, all his words caught in his throat, and he couldn't utter a sound.

In the end, Lin Bei could only sigh inwardly.

"Sigh, let it be."

"I can't bear to stop it."

"Its eyes, so determined, like it's going to XXX..."

"If it's a supercar, it's a supercar. I don't mind being a little wronged."

He even felt like he was being kind.

...

The crowd erupted in uproar.

They never imagined that this multi-million dollar luxury car was just the beginning?

"That's outrageous! Driving a supercar like a taxi? Have you ever seen such an extravagant taxi?"

"We're all human, why is there such a huge difference in treatment? I drive a beat-up car with incomplete doors, but he drives a car worth millions! The ridiculous thing is, this is just a temporary vehicle!"

"I'm really curious who he is and why he gets such special treatment. That monster is clearly trying to curry favor with him, more obsequious than a simp!"

"..."

The vehicle manager ignored the others.

Its gaze remained fixed on Lin Bei.

"Brother, when do you plan to start work?"

"I can help you with other things, but this is something you have to do yourself."

"Don't overthink it. It's not that I don't want to help, but I can't. You're a participant in a professional challenge, and there are supervisors watching you in secret. You must abide by the basic rules of this round of the challenge."

"Understand?"

Lin Bei nodded.

He'd heard of this kind of thing before.

Once selected, the most basic requirement is to follow the rules. To become a taxi driver, one must drive a taxi and pick up passengers; to become a chef, one must chop vegetables and cook.

As for how to pick up passengers, where to take them, or what kind of vegetables to chop and how to chop them, these are not strictly regulated and don't need to be strictly followed, nor will they be considered violations by the regulators.

"I understand."

"Without further ado, I plan to start working now."

The vehicle supervisor naturally offered his full support.

It promptly began making arrangements.

"Let's get to work!"

"Drive as much as you want, pick up as many passengers as you want."

"There's only one requirement: earn at least 300 Terror Coins, the more the better, of course."

"Food and drink are not included, take care of yourself."

"Each car has a different amount of gas left, but it's definitely enough for 100-200 kilometers. Once it's used up, you'll have to pay for the gas yourself."

After saying that, the vehicle manager turned to Lin Bei and said:

"Of course, you don't need to worry about that."

"This car just had a full tank of gas, and there are plenty of snacks and drinks in the car. There's also 1000 Terror Coins in the armrest box. Eat and use as you like, do whatever makes you happy."

"But remember, you must earn at least 300 Terror Coins."

Lin Bei nodded slightly, indicating that he understood, and didn't say anything more.

The vehicle manager had done enough; he was satisfied.

The others, hearing this, felt another wave of resentment.

Vroom!

Soon, the twelve taxis left the parking lot one after another.

...

After Lin Bei and the others left, the vehicle manager returned to his office, took out a cell phone, and dialed a number.

Beep~

Beep~~

The call was quickly answered, and a sweet voice rang out:

"Hello?"

"Xiao Hong?"

"Why are you calling me all of a sudden?"

The vehicle manager's name is Traffic Light, so calling him Little Red is quite reasonable.

Moreover, the fact that they dare to call the vehicle manager Little Red confirms that the person on the other end is definitely not ordinary.

"Boss, I'd like to request three days off."

"Because I have something very important to do, no matter what difficulties I encounter, I must do it."

"Please grant my request!"

"If you don't grant it, then I will resign!"

The vehicle manager's tone was firmer than ever before.

It spoke politely, in a gentle and persuasive manner, but its words brooked no refusal, conveying only one meaning.

These three days off, you must grant them!

Don't grant them?

Fine!

I, Traffic Light, no longer want you as my boss!

On the other end, the strange woman who claimed to be the boss remained silent for a long time.

This news shocked her.

She could sense the vehicle manager's determination; nothing could stop it from doing this.

After a long pause, the female boss finally gave her answer:

"I can approve it."

"But on the condition that you tell me what you're going to do with these three days off?"

"You've worked for me for over ten years; I'll try my best to help you if I can."

"I'm not just curious."

Actually, she was genuinely curious.

She had known Traffic Light for years and knew exactly what kind of strange person he was. Normally, Traffic Light would spend the entire day at the Abyss Taxi Company, taking care of all his meals, drinking, and other needs there, and wouldn't go out for fun after getting paid.

But now, he was suddenly making this request.

It was truly unbelievable; there must be a significant reason behind it, and she couldn't help but be interested.

The vehicle manager didn't try to hide anything.

"Because I met the most important brother I've ever had."

"He's an intern at the company, very interested in supercars and such. One of his dreams is to drive a supercar as a taxi."

"Although he didn't say it, I know that's what he's thinking, so I'm going to fulfill my brother's wish and get him a supercar as a gift."

"Boss, don't try to persuade me, don't stop me, my mind is made up!"

It didn't think this was absurd.

To it, everything was normal.

The female boss was stunned after hearing this.

"Are you crazy?!"

"Doing something like this for an intern?"

"And a male intern?"

"Does Little Red like male ghosts?"

"Hiss~"

"Isn't this just pure simping?"



Chapter 5 System Upgrade, First Lottery Draw


Despite her thoughts, the female boss didn't directly confront her, but instead earnestly advised:

"Calm down, don't be impulsive."

"Think it over carefully. Is it really necessary to go to this extent for a male ghost you just met?"

"With your assets..."

But before she could finish, the vehicle manager ruthlessly interrupted her.

"It's worth it!"

"Don't even mention a supercar; even if he asked for the stars and the moon, I'd do everything in my power to get them for him."

"Also, my brother is human."

"Please respect him, boss. Don't confuse his race."

"Boss, you probably think I'm a simp now, right?"

"Actually, you're wrong. My orientation is normal. I'm helping him not because I like him, nor to seduce him, but because of our brotherhood, forged through life and death together."

"That's it. I'm going to take action."

Then, the phone was hung up.

Ms. Yueying, the owner of the Abyss Taxi Company, remained in the position of answering the phone.

Her understanding was drastically shattered.

She never imagined the traffic light would go to such lengths for a human.

"I've only been gone a few days, how did it become like this?"

"Bewitched by a human, it can no longer maintain its rationality."

"He's ruthless!"

If the root cause were a male ghost, Ms. Yueying could barely accept it.

But the root cause is a human, and a man at that; she truly cannot accept it.

"I need to finish what I'm doing and go back to check on things."

"I have a bad feeling."

"Little Red has inexplicably become like this, will the other ghosts be affected as well?"

"If so, the situation will be terrible, and I'll have to step in to solve the problem."

"Or perhaps..."

"Just kill this human?"

Ms. Yueying was always ruthless.

If Lin Bei was in trouble, she was truly willing to kill him!

...

Lin Bei drove the "All Die - Shadow" through a main street in the horror world.

The car's interior was bathed in blue ambient lighting, and a top-of-the-line sound system played soothing music. The seats were incredibly soft and included a massage function, making them extremely comfortable. The accelerator and brakes were exceptionally responsive, reacting to the slightest touch.

He drove casually, observing his surroundings.

Many strange creatures were pacing along the roadside.

Several of these creatures spotted Lin Bei's luxury car and uniformly revealed envious and jealous looks. A few even gasped in surprise.

"Holy crap, all of you die—Shadow Spirit, that's awesome!"

"A taxi...? Am I seeing things? Using Shadow Spirit as a taxi? That's incredible! I could work my whole life and still not afford this kind of hearse."

"Who can afford a taxi like that? A single ride would cost at least four figures, right?"

"While I do need a taxi, I simply don't have the confidence to ride in this one."

"..."

Luxury car, music, night view, eerie envious glances from the roadside...

All of this felt unreal to Lin Bei, making him wonder if he was really participating in a horror game.

This treatment, this experience, he'd never experienced anything like it in real life.

"That's really awesome."

"And this only increased the favorability of an ordinary ghost."

“What if it were a very powerful anomaly?”

“For example, the owner of a taxi company, or the ruler of this eerie city?”

“What would that be like?”

Lin Bei didn’t dare to think about it.

Because he simply couldn’t imagine it.

“System, how many experience points have you reached?”

“I’ve been out for a while.”

The system panel popped up.

【System Level: lv1..】

【Affected Levels: Wandering Souls and below】

【Experience Points: 1000/1000】

【Experience has reached its limit and cannot be increased further. Increase level?】

【New features will be unlocked after leveling up.】

【If you confirm leveling up, the current target's favorability will be stopped.】

Lin Bei didn’t hesitate and made a decisive decision.

"Level up."

"Terminate the favorability increase for the traffic light."

"990 favorability points."

"That's enough."

[Ding!]

[System level has been increased to lv2!]

[System Level: lv2..]

[Affected by: Wraiths and below]

[System Functions: Favorability Increase, Lucky Roulette..]

[Experience Points: 0/10000]

[Upon reaching lv3, the portable space function will be unlocked!]

Lin Bei's lips twitched, a bitter look flashing in his eyes.

"Ten thousand experience points?"

"Ten times..."

"That's quite difficult."

"Let's try the lucky wheel first."

He was really looking forward to the lucky wheel feature.

After all, in a horror world, strength still matters.

Powerful monsters are everywhere, and the system's current level is still low, plus it can only be used on one target at a time.

[The lucky wheel has five levels.]

[Bronze, Silver, Gold, Diamond, Legendary.]

[Each draw requires a certain amount of favor points, from lowest to highest: 100 points, 500 points, 5000 points, 25000 points, 200000 points...]

[Each draw guarantees a reward, and a guaranteed reward is given after ten draws.]

[Would you like to spin the lucky wheel?]

[Current favor points: 1000!]

Lin Bei quickly figured out what was going on.

To be honest, he wasn't too surprised.

This kind of lucky wheel feature with different levels and a guaranteed reward mechanism is perfectly normal.

What pleased Lin Bei most was that every draw yielded a reward; he never received anything like "thank you for your patronage."

"1000 points, just enough for a ten-draw."

"System."

"Ten-draw on the Bronze Roulette."

The next moment, a bronze roulette wheel appeared in Lin Bei's mind.

The roulette wheel was large, with hundreds of rewards on it.

Most emitted a grayish-white light, with only a very few emitting a golden, red, or purple glow.

A huge pointer spun rapidly.

A few seconds later, it stopped on a grayish-white area.

[Received a pack of compressed biscuits.]

The second time was the same.

[Received a bottle of insect repellent.]

[Received a pair of sunglasses.]

[Received a roasted chicken.]

[...]

Nine times in a row, all ordinary rewards.

The tenth time, the pointer stopped in the red area.

[Congratulations on winning a rare reward.]

[You have obtained the divine skill - Masterful Driver!]

[The divine skill has been fused...]

[Your driving skills have reached the highest level. You have mastered all driving techniques, and all theoretical knowledge is memorized.]

[You have memorized the entire map of the horror world, including highways, city roads, and country lanes. Just mention a place name, and you will know how to get there.]

A vast amount of information appeared in Lin Bei's mind—a super map containing the entire horror world, from a deadly, eerie city to a nameless village.

It's no exaggeration to say that his brain has become the most advanced and useful navigation system!

"It's a great reward."

"Perfectly useful!"



Chapter 6, First Passenger: S-Class Fugitive


Lin Bei was fairly satisfied with the results of his first ten-pull.

Although the guaranteed item wasn't a combat-related item, it would still be quite useful.

"My points are now zero."

"I need to accumulate more points."

"I'll accumulate more this time."

"The higher the roulette wheel's level, the better the items you can get."

Lin Bei checked the other roulette wheels.

The Bronze roulette wheel mostly contained gray-white orbs, occupying 95% of the empty slots, with green and blue at 4%, and the remaining 1% being gold and red.

The Silver roulette wheel was a significant improvement in quality; the gray-white orbs decreased by half, and many green and blue orbs appeared, with a considerable increase in purple and gold.

The Gold roulette wheel had no gray-white orbs; blue and green orbs occupied half, with a large increase in purple orbs, followed by gold and red.

The Diamond roulette wheel mostly contained purple orbs, with gold and red orbs evenly split.

The Legendary Roulette wheel is visibly dominated by golden and red orbs, making up almost 90% of the rewards. The remaining 10% consists of rainbow orbs, the lowest being red.

Grayish-white represents common rewards.

Blue and green represent average and good rewards, respectively.

Purple represents rare rewards.

Red represents precious rewards.

Gold represents divine rewards.

Rare orbs represent the highest-level rewards!

"It seems that even a casual spin on the Legendary Roulette yields better rewards than the guaranteed reward on the Bronze Roulette."

"Although the points conversion is significantly lower, the Bronze Roulette has far too few good rewards, less than ten in total."

"And there are rainbow rewards, corresponding to the highest-level rewards."

"Take it slow."

He really wanted to do a ten-pull on the Legendary Roulette.

But the problem is it's too expensive, requiring millions of points...

Out of reach!

...

Lin Bei didn't pay much attention to the lucky roulette wheel.

Now, he possessed a divine skill; his driving skills had improved dramatically. Before, he had to grip the steering wheel tightly with both hands, but now he held it with one hand, casually driving forward and occasionally overtaking other cars.

"Why isn't anyone stopping my car?"

"This is a taxi!"

Lin Bei found this very annoying.

He had been wandering around outside for half an hour, but not a single strange creature had hailed a cab.

He happened to come across a red light, so he gently pressed the accelerator and looked across the street.

Just then, a taxi was parked across the street.

Uh...

It was a very dilapidated taxi, with dented doors, smashed headlights, and mud and bird droppings all over the body.

Through the window, he could see that the person sitting in the driver's seat was clearly a human—one of the people who had chosen the car with him earlier.

It was clearly a beat-up car, yet a strange figure carrying a chainsaw walked up to it, opened the back door, got in, and exchanged a few words with the person inside, clearly heading somewhere.

"Huh?"

"This kind of car can actually pick up customers?"

"I'm so envious!"

"I should have driven a beat-up car."

He felt a tiny bit of regret.

Lin Bei glanced at the strange creature in the back seat, and its information panel appeared.

【Chainsaw Maniac】

【Affection Points: 0】

[Relationship: Stranger]

[Level: Cunning]

【

He is a passionate chainsaw enthusiast, with an extreme love for chainsaws.

No matter where it went or what it did, the chainsaw was always by its side.

It felt uncomfortable if it didn't kill someone or do something bizarre with its chainsaw every day.

Today...

Its chainsaw wasn't even started yet!

】

After reading this, Lin Bei felt a chill run down his spine.

His gaze towards the man changed.

"Good luck."

He suddenly felt no envy.

Taking a customer?

This was clearly taking in a living devil!

Just then, the chainsaw maniac sitting in the back row suddenly looked up and met Lin Bei's gaze.

Then, its mouth stretched to both sides, revealing a chilling smile, exposing teeth full of cavities and bits of flesh.

At the same time, it pointed a finger at Lin Bei.

A beam of blood appeared, transforming into a bloody line that seeped into Lin Bei's body.

The information panel appeared again.

[Chainsaw Maniac]

[Affection Points: -50]

[Relationship: Hunter and Prey]

[Level: Vicious]

[

You're next!

Killing you and selling your car can buy countless chainsaws.

Hehe...

It's already marked you.

You can't escape its pursuit even if you run to the ends of the earth.

]

Lin Bei was speechless.

Was it really necessary?

Just one glance!

A replica of the horror world, what are you looking at?

He truly experienced the horror of the horror world now.

Just a casual glance, and he'd brought about his death.

"Its level is 'Fierce Ghost'."

"The system is only level two; we can't increase its favorability for now."

"Upgrading requires 10,000 experience points, which we can't do in a short time."

"We need to think of another way."

Lin Bei frowned deeply.

He had no doubt about the system's accuracy; the traffic light example proved everything.

The chainsaw maniac would make its move by tomorrow at the latest, after all, one a day.

The taxi across the street started moving, carrying the chainsaw maniac away.

Everything seemed to have returned to normal.

But Lin Bei felt heavy-hearted.

"Sometimes, driving a luxury car isn't a good thing; it makes you vulnerable to being targeted by ghosts."

As he finished speaking, the red light turned green.

Lin Bei lightly pressed the accelerator and drove across the street.

Just after crossing the intersection, he noticed a ghost wearing a black raincoat waving dozens of meters away.

Its hands were very white, a sickly pale, with nails painted jet black.

Other than that, he couldn't discern anything else.

"Oh?"

"Need a ride?"

Lin Bei's gaze shifted, focusing mainly on the face, trying to make out what the eerie creature looked like.

But he couldn't see it.

Even with streetlights on and the blood-red moonlight spilling onto the ground, his vision seemed blocked by some force; he couldn't make out the face beneath the raincoat.

However, Lin Bei saw the information panel!

【Rain Demon】

【Affection Points: -500】

[Relationship: Either you or her]

[Level: Vengeful Spirit]

【

She is a fugitive, an S-class mysterious wanted criminal!

Although only at the Wraith level, when combined with its abilities, it can kill a Fierce Ghost level monster, and even fight to a draw with an Evil Ghost level monster. In optimal conditions, it can even rival a Ghost King level monster.

She's got her eye on you!

She hates you to the core!

The reason is simple: it's not that you offended her, but that she equally hates every wealthy monster and human.

The richer they are, the more she hates them!

Before she met you, she wandered around, targeting any wealthy person she saw.

Being targeted by her basically meant certain death.

You...

You're finished!!

】

Lin Bei slammed on the brakes.

Don't pull!

Absolutely don't pull!

This money can be forfeited, but your life must be saved!

He unleashed the power of a master driver, the steering wheel spinning rapidly, gears shifting seamlessly, making a U-turn on the spot, flooring the accelerator, ready to leave as quickly as possible.

The numbers on the dashboard jumped from two digits to three.

Lin Bei gripped the steering wheel tightly, not daring to relax for a moment.

At the same time, he kept checking the rearview mirror.

He saw that the terrifying, eerie creature named Yu Mei was still standing there, only its raised hand had been lowered, and it was watching the direction he had left in.

"It should be alright now."

Whoosh!

Suddenly, a torrential downpour began.

The foul-smelling raindrops pelted the car window.

Within seconds, his vision was severely impaired, making it impossible to see anything ahead.

"Why did it suddenly start raining?"

A bad feeling crept into Lin Bei's heart.

He turned on the windshield wipers at their highest setting.

The wipers swept the raindrops aside, barely restoring Lin Bei's vision.

Then, he witnessed a chilling scene.

The eerie creature called Rain Demon had appeared a meter in front of the car, standing in the rain, staring intently at Lin Bei.

It reappeared in the information panel.

【Rain Demon】

【Affection Points: -600】

【Relationship: I'll make you die in the rain】

【Level: Vengeful Spirit】

【

It's raining, the game has begun!】

】



Chapter 7 Destination: Mass Gravesite; News Playing Inside the Vehicle


"Holy crap!"

"My favorability dropped another 100 points?"

"What the hell?!"

Lin Bei had already reacted, instinctively wanting to slam on the brakes.

But then he thought again.

Stop what the hell?

My favorability points have already dropped by 600.

Stopping is practically suicide, isn't it?

I might as well be ruthless, give it my all, and hopefully kill that weirdo in front of me. That way, I can at least get rid of some trouble.

So, Lin Bei stepped on the gas.

Vroom!

The car's speed suddenly increased, heading straight for the female weirdo, Yu Mei.

But an astonishing scene unfolded: the front of the car didn't hit Yu Mei; instead, it passed right through her body and continued rushing forward.

Rain once again filled the windshield.

The wipers continued to operate, and Yu Mei's figure reappeared, still a meter away.

Whoosh!

Lin Bei continued to press the gas.

But the result was the same; he couldn't hit her at all.

The information panel automatically appeared.

【Rain Charm】

【Favorability Points: -666】

【..】

“It’s gone down again…”

The car continued moving, crashing again and again, each time with the same result, as if it had encountered a peculiar, unpredictable obstacle.

Clearly, the plan had failed.

This strange creature was determined to get in the car.

Seeing this, Lin Bei stepped on the brakes.

A few seconds later, the car stopped.

Rain Charm, who had been standing in front, walked step by step to the passenger door, reached out and grabbed the door handle, pulling hard. The locked door seemed to malfunction, and was pulled open from the outside.

Half of Rain Charm's body sat inside the car, rainwater soaking the seat and splashing everywhere inside.

She looked at Lin Bei, her tone cold and sharp.

"Why did you turn around?"

"That's refusing service, a fatal mistake."

"Why did you hit me?"

"You clearly saw me!"

At this moment, Lin Bei finally saw Yu Mei's face hidden under her raincoat.

His first impression was one of innocence.

An innocent, harmless innocence.

Like the girl next door waiting for the bus on a rainy summer night, youthful, cute, and beautiful, inspiring an impulse to have many stories with her.

But when he saw those eyes, as black as night, that feeling vanished instantly.

Those eyes were filled with malice, eyes that would devour someone without spitting out the bones.

"It's a misunderstanding. I didn't refuse a ride; I was just planning to turn around anyway."

"As for why I hit you?"

"That's your own fault!"

"Why did you suddenly appear in front of my car? In this heavy rain, I couldn't see anything ahead and didn't have time to brake."

At this point, Lin Bei knew running away was useless, so he chose to confront it head-on.

Besides, he was in the right.

【Rain Charm】

【Favorability Points: -700】

【..】

【

She's very angry.

She wants to crush you.

She just wants to take you to the secret base and deal with you in the most cruel way.

】

".."

Lin Bei was speechless.

Is it so hard to admit you're wrong?

..

Rain Charm didn't intend to say anything more about it.

"The Mass Graveyard of Shattered Bones."

"Let's go now."

"Or do you want to kick me off?"

Her tone was sinister, her eyes flashing with cold light.

She was willing to kill Lin Bei on the spot if he dared to refuse her a ride.

Lin Bei, of course, wouldn't refuse.

Because he had his eye on Yu Mei.

Since she'd come to him of her own accord, it wouldn't be right to do nothing.

"Without a doubt, she's strong; her power is related to rain."

"The information panel says she can kill monsters stronger than herself, and at her best, she can overcome three levels. She's a great henchman, useful against the Chainsaw Maniac."

"Most importantly, her level is Wraith, within the range where favorability can be increased."

"Although the favorability is a bit low..."

Phew~

Taking a deep breath, Lin Bei's state returned to normal.

He turned off the wipers and said with a smile:

"Okay."

"Let's go."

At the same time, he communicated with the system in his mind.

"System."

"The new target is her. Increase her favorability."

[Ding!]

[Target locked!]

[Favorability has begun to increase.]

Lin Bei drove normally.

The Bone Shattering Massacre wasn't far from here, a five-minute drive at most.

"Don't drive too fast."

"This madman's favorability is too low; it'll take more than ten minutes just to zero it out."

"It might even drop further during that time."

"So we need to stall a bit."

Yu Mei sat in the passenger seat.

The car was already moving, but she wasn't looking ahead. Instead, she kept her head tilted, staring at Lin Bei, her eyes filled with undisguised resentment and murderous intent.

If looks could kill, Lin Bei would have died a hundred times over.

Lin Bei, however, kept his eyes glued to the road as he drove.

The radio continued playing music, mostly eerie songs, interspersed with occasional news clips.

"Master, is this your first day on the job?"

"Have you heard the story of the Rain Night Butcher?"

Yu Mei suddenly asked.

Lin Bei frowned slightly, but quickly regained his composure.

"No."

He truly hadn't heard of it.

"Want to hear it?"

"No."

[Rain Charm]

[Affection Points: -700]

His affection, which had recovered considerably, plummeted back to -700.

Lin Bei immediately corrected himself:

"I misspoke."

"Actually, I want to hear it."

Only then did Rain Charm make a new move, reaching out to switch the car radio.

Finally, she switched to radio station number 444.

This was a very special computer, broadcasting various dangerous, wanted, and bizarre news.

'SS-level wanted: Mysterious Snow Woman, bounty of five million Terror Coins. Characterized by long white hair, a preference for white clothing with snowflake patterns, and is usually accompanied by falling snow wherever she goes. Her strength is at the Disaster level...'

'SS-level wanted: Mysterious Three-Headed Hound, bounty of five million Terror Coins. Characterized by a large dog with three heads...'

'SS-level wanted: Mysterious...'

'S-level wanted: Mysterious...'

'S-level...'

'...'

'...'

'S-level wanted: Mysterious Rain Night Butcher, bounty of two million Terror Coins. Characterized by wearing a black raincoat, preferring to go out at night, committing indiscriminate crimes with extremely cruel methods, and possessing abilities related to rain. Please stay away from female mysteries wearing black raincoats. Reliable sources indicate she resides in Gray Abyss City year-round.'

Lin Bei roughly understood what Yu Mei was trying to do.

She was clearly trying to scare him this way.

Just then, Yu Mei's voice reached her ears.

"Wearing a black raincoat, going out at night, living in Gray Abyss City."

"Driver..."

"Do I match these three characteristics?"

Lin Bei stared intently ahead, nodding obligingly.

"Very much."

"I almost thought you were the wanted criminal!"

"That was terrifying!"

He caught Yu Mei's reaction out of the corner of his eye.

Yu Mei smiled contentedly.

"Tell me, is there a possibility?"

"What if I am that S-level wanted criminal?"

"And I got into your car because I wanted to kill you?"

"After all, the place I'm going to is a mass grave!"



Chapter 8: This is professionalism. Remember to give it a good review.


Lin Bei answered seriously:

"That makes sense."

"All the characteristics match."

"It seems you really are this S-class wanted criminal."

"So, after I get you to your destination, you'll kill me, right?"

His tone was calm, showing little fear, as if he wasn't the one about to suffer.

Meanwhile, Lin Bei was focused on driving, strictly adhering to traffic rules, even though this was a horror world...

"Aren't you afraid?"

Yu Mei didn't understand Lin Bei's behavior.

This wasn't the first time she'd used this method.

She enjoyed letting her targets die in extreme fear and despair!

Previously, she used it in a sealed environment, where the target, knowing her identity, had nowhere to escape, only experiencing endless panic, thus displaying all sorts of ugly behavior, desperately trying to survive, begging for mercy, and offering huge sums of money to buy her life...

Yu Mei enjoyed this feeling.

This once aloof and wealthy woman, now begging for mercy like a pitiful wretch, had become addicted to this after a few experiences.

Lin Bei answered truthfully.

"I'm scared!"

"You're going to kill me, how could I not be scared?"

Yu Mei was taken aback.

She hadn't seen any sign of fear in Lin Bei.

"If you're scared, why don't you run?"

"You have a chance to jump out of the car."

Yu Mei didn't actually want Lin Bei to escape.

She said this because she wanted to torture and toy with him.

She wanted Lin Bei to panic and run away, only to get lost in the torrential rain each time, subjecting him to both physical and psychological torment until he inevitably broke down.

Like a cat that catches a mouse but doesn't eat it immediately, instead toying with it for a long time, repeatedly creating the illusion that the mouse could survive, before ruthlessly destroying it.

Yes, she was a psychopath!

"Why are you running away?"

"It's your business if you want to kill me."

"My duty is to get you to your destination."

"Once you're in my car, you're my passenger, and I have to complete this fare."

"It's my professional ethics!"

"Remember to give me a good review."

He was obviously lying.

If it weren't for the system, he would have jumped out of the car long ago.

He's staying in the car now only because he's confident; given enough time, Yu Mei's favorability rating will rise.

Lin Bei has made his decision.

Once his favorability rating is high enough, he'll get his revenge.

Dare to scare him?

Dare to have murderous thoughts?

Dare to torment him?

These will all come at a price!

"Even if you cry and apologize, I won't be soft-hearted."

"An S-level wanted criminal, huh?"

"I'll turn you into an SSS-level crybaby!"

...

Yu Mei was clearly shocked by Lin Bei's answer, freezing in her seat, her dark eyes glazed over.

She was dumbfounded!

A person who drives a million-dollar luxury car like a taxi, yet possesses such professionalism?

Knowing it's a dead end, he still insists on completing this fare?

He's insane!

Completely different from the other wealthy people she'd met before.

"6!"

After this incident, Yu Mei knew that this approach wouldn't scare Lin Bei.

So she simply sat up straight, half-reclining in her chair, her dark eyes closed, and began to consider how to deal with Lin Bei.

"What should I do?"

"There are two more wealthy scoundrels locked up in the dungeon."

"How about locking them all up together, then telling them I'll spare the last one who survives, letting the three of them fight to the death?"

"Or how about cutting them all into pieces?"

"Drowning them in the rain seems like a good idea too."

"I'd also like to try burying them alive."

"..."

At this moment, she hadn't realized the serious consequences these thoughts would have.

The more ways she thought of torturing Lin Bei, the more ruthless the methods she used, the stronger her guilt would become afterward, and the harder it would be to gain Lin Bei's forgiveness.

...

At this moment, Lin Bei glanced at Yu Mei out of the corner of his eye.

The information bar immediately appeared.

【Yu Mei】

【Affection Points: -330】

【...】

【

Her hatred for you has lessened somewhat.

But she still wants to kill you!

She's currently considering how to kill you.

】

Lin Bei breathed a sigh of relief, his inner unease quickly dissipating.

"Not bad."

"It's already halved."

"We've covered almost half the distance."

"Almost there..."

...

Meanwhile, in another taxi.

The chainsaw maniac sat in the back, clutching a gleaming chainsaw.

It could clearly sense Lin Bei's location and condition.

"Is there a vengeful spirit-level monster beside me?"

"Keep going."

"You must survive."

"My beloved chainsaw wants to slice you to pieces."

No one wanted Lin Bei to live more than the chainsaw maniac.

In the front, Bai Zhou stepped on the brakes, pulling the car over to the side of the road.

"Hello...we've arrived at our destination."

"The fare is twenty...twenty Terror Coins?"

"How would you like to pay?"

He didn't really want to speak.

But he had no choice; it was part of the service.

The chainsaw maniac immediately started the chainsaw.

"How will you pay?"

"Your life, of course!"

It grinned, raising its chainsaw to strike Bai Zhou.

"Ah!"

And this was just the beginning.

"Baby, are you satisfied?"

"If not, let's continue!"

The chainsaw flashed a dim red light.

"Not satisfied?"

"Okay!"

"Continue!"

Clang!

Clang!

...

'Class B wanted: Bizarre Chainsaw Maniac, bounty of 500,000 Terror Coins. Characteristics include a penchant for running around with a chainsaw, a strong physique, a ferocious appearance, and indiscriminate attacks daily. Some sources claim it's a puppet controlled by the chainsaw...'

Lin Bei didn't switch radios and heard the message.

"It's also a wanted monster?"

The next moment, the soil beneath her feet shifted to both sides, revealing an underground passage.

A flicker of surprise crossed Lin Bei's eyes.

This underground passage was ingeniously designed; it couldn't have been built without specialized knowledge and skills.

"Yu Mei, did you build this dungeon?"

He asked directly.

He knew Yu Mei would tell the truth.

"No, I didn't."

"A good friend of mine built it."

"She's an SS-level wanted criminal; her specialty is creating all sorts of traps and mechanisms."

"Many supernatural beings hire her to build dungeons and such."

Upon hearing this, Lin Bei frowned.

Another wanted supernatural being!

"This time it's an SS-level wanted criminal?"

"Chainsaw Maniac, Rain Demon, this supernatural being..."

"That's strange."

"They're wanted, yet they still dare to roam around?"

"Something feels off."



Chapter 13 An Extremely Dangerous Profession, A Trembling Feeling


Although puzzled, Lin Bei didn't ask now.

He planned to ask later.

This environment wasn't suitable for asking such questions.

After all, this was a world where the eerie was everywhere!

...

The staircase was quite long, at least thirty meters high.

The walls on both sides were inlaid with some kind of special glass spheres that continuously emitted a dark red light. Even more strangely, the damp, bloody smell was completely blocked, as if entering a dungeon a thousand miles away.

Moreover, there was a floral fragrance in the dungeon; even standing within a meter of the tombstone, he hadn't smelled this scent.

"How interesting."

"Air circulation isn't affected, yet it effectively blocks the stench; the outside smell can't get in, and the inside smell can't get out."

"And what about the sound?"

"What's going on now?"

Yu Mei, gritting her teeth as she descended the stairs ahead, seemed to sense Lin Bei's confusion.

"This dungeon is specifically designed to torture wealthy and bizarre individuals, so it has several unique design features."

"First, the smells and air inside and outside don't affect each other."

"Second, sound can enter, but it won't travel out."

Lin Bei nodded knowingly, his interest in the SS-level wanted bizarre creature growing even stronger.

If given the chance, he would try to make contact with this creature.

Clearly, this creature's power was very useful.

Bringing a creature with such special powers to his side would definitely not be a bad thing; as long as he raised his favor, he wouldn't have to worry about betrayal.

"I need to take advantage of the fact that I don't have to challenge any dangerous professions right now and focus on dealing with as many strange and unusual creatures as possible."

Even with a system, Lin Bei never underestimated the profession challenge game.

He knew very well that

challenging those high-difficulty professions would inevitably involve extremely dangerous battles—the kind where one wrong move could mean death!

"The higher the difficulty of the challenged profession, the better the reward."

"It's said that someone, after completing the Ghostly Messenger profession challenge, successfully obtained the Ghostly Messenger's exclusive ability, allowing them to forcibly extract the soul of their target."

As long as I keep surviving, encountering a high-difficulty profession is only a matter of time.

A while later, Lin Bei officially entered the dungeon.

The structure was relatively simple.

Five cells and a large hall.

【Rotting Flesh Fetus - Fat Snake】

[

Chapter 15: Money Counts, Rain Charm Knows the Truth


At this moment, both the Carrion Embryo Monster Fat Snake and the Giant Strength Monster Akin placed their hopes on Lin Bei.

The kind that explodes like crazy!!

For daily necessities like food, drink, and shelter, after the challenge ends, you'll go to a safe zone, but only accommodation is free in the safe zone; food and drinks all require Terror Coins.

Chapter 17 The Figure Holding an Umbrella in the Downpour


Without a doubt, the Death Banquet Oil-Paper Umbrella is incredibly powerful!

“An S-rank Netherworld Artifact.”

“E-rank, D-rank, C-rank, B-rank, A-rank, S-rank, SS-rank, SSS-rank…”

“If the Silver Roulette can draw an S-rank reward, drawing an SS-rank or SSS-rank reward shouldn't be too difficult.”

“On the other hand, if an S-rank Netherworld Artifact is already this powerful, then wouldn't an SS-rank Netherworld Artifact be even more powerful? And an SSS-rank Netherworld Artifact, of course, goes without saying.”

Lin Bei doesn't plan to keep this umbrella for himself.

Although he doesn't currently have any other Netherworld Artifacts.

But clearly, this Netherworld Artifact is more suitable for Yu Mei to use.

He instinctively feels a slight aversion to this umbrella.

After all, holding that umbrella is like holding a swarm of insects.

Countless wet, ruthless insects!

“Once I get my hands on those two terrifying coins, I need to buy some artifacts or abilities from the Horror Shop. I also need to hurry up and become stronger before I come into contact with those dangerous professions.”

“Otherwise, besides the system, I have no way to deal with the supernatural beings.”

“The system level is still too low; it can only be used on one supernatural being at a time. I can't rely entirely on the system.”

“Besides, I don't want the supernatural beings to suspect me of being a kept man…”

Lin Bei knew one thing well.

Improving his own strength was definitely the right thing to do.

Even with supernatural beings like Yu Mei protecting him, he couldn't be protected forever.

“System, are the rewards I can get from the roulette wheel the same as the items in the Horror Shop?”

【No!】

【Items in the Horror Shop can be obtained by spinning the lucky wheel, but many rewards in the lucky wheel are unique and cannot be purchased in the Horror Shop or obtained through other means.】

[Buy whatever you want, host. You won't get duplicate rewards when you spin the roulette wheel later.]

Lin Bei nodded, saying nothing more.

His resolve was even stronger.

He had to buy something.

Anyway, he didn't have to worry about drawing something he'd already bought.

...

The moment Lin Bei held the Death Banquet oil-paper umbrella in his hand, Yu Mei, who was alone and regretting her actions, sensed something.

She subconsciously looked at the umbrella in Lin Bei's hand.

This umbrella gave her a very familiar yet terrifying feeling.

It smelled of rain.

It exuded the aura of death.

Looking directly at the umbrella, she could even hear wails and screams.

Yu Mei's attention was completely drawn to the umbrella; she couldn't look away.

"What an amazing umbrella..."

"It's a very high-grade Netherworld artifact!"

"This umbrella exudes a dangerous aura, making me feel fear and unease."

Cold sweat beaded on her forehead, and her entire body tensed involuntarily.

More than anything, Yu Mei was most worried about Lin Bei. She wasn't sure if he could suppress the umbrella.

After all, this Netherworld artifact was too powerful!

For an ordinary person with almost no strength, possessing a Netherworld artifact of this level wasn't necessarily a good thing.

If one's strength couldn't gain the Netherworld artifact's approval, there was a risk of being devoured by it.

Yu Mei walked to Lin Bei's side.

She had made up her mind.

If Lin Bei were to open this umbrella and encounter mortal danger, she would immediately shield him, taking the brunt of the danger for him.

No matter the cost, even certain death, she would protect Lin Bei.

Seeing Yu Mei approach, Lin Bei looked at her.

The information panel appeared automatically.

【Yu Mei】

【Affection Points: 4344】

【Relationship: Unwavering love and loyalty + The deeper the love, the heavier the guilt + Willing to die for you】

【Level: Vengeful Spirit】

【

She's worried about you!

She's afraid you'll get hurt.

If you encounter danger, she will unhesitatingly shield you, using her own life to protect you.

She wouldn't let you die even if it meant her own.

】

Lin Bei instantly understood the situation.

He smiled and shook his head.

“This girl.”

“Giving her the umbrella was the right thing to do.”

Lin Bei was satisfied with Yu Mei.

He was truly in love and loyal!

To the point where there was no room for doubt!

He decisively handed the Death Banquet oil-paper umbrella to Yu Mei.

At the same time, he smiled and said:

"Take it."

"This umbrella is yours from now on."

"Death Banquet Oil-Paper Umbrella, S-Rank Netherworld Artifact."

"Composed of countless rain insects."

"Study the rest yourself."

"It suits you very well."

Yu Mei blankly accepted the Death Banquet Oil-Paper Umbrella.

For a moment, her mind couldn't process it.

An S-Rank Netherworld Artifact...

Just given to her like that?

This can't be real!

Does he think this is a ten-dollar umbrella from a roadside shop?

This is an S-Rank Netherworld Artifact!

An S-Rank Netherworld Artifact!!

A synonym for rarity and power. Let alone a vengeful spirit like her, even a catastrophic or nightmare-level anomaly might not possess an S-Rank Netherworld Artifact.

"Given to...given to me?"

"I can't...I can't accept it!"

"It's too precious!"

"I already feel so guilty towards you, having done so many bad things to you. I haven't even made amends for the mistakes I made, how can I accept this S-grade artifact?"

Yu Mei felt she wasn't worthy of it.

Whether it was an S-grade or a D-grade artifact, she wouldn't accept it.

Simply because of her guilt.

"I said it's for you, so take it."

"If you refuse again, I will never forgive you for the rest of my life."

Now, Yu Mei was even more at a loss.

Take it?

She felt unworthy.

Don't take it?

Lin Bei would blame her for the rest of her life.

"I'll count to three!"

"One..."

Lin Bei used his ultimate move.

Yu Mei panicked immediately.

"I want it!"

How could she dare refuse?

The legend of counting to three also circulated in the supernatural world.

Yu Mei accepted the Death Banquet oil-paper umbrella.

The moment her hand touched the handle, she felt an intimacy, as if the umbrella were an extension of her own body.

She silently stepped aside and began to examine this S-grade funerary artifact.

...

Soon, the competition between the two ghosts, Fat Snake and A Jin, came to a complete end.

They had both borrowed several million.

The outcome was reversed.

A Jin was the one who ultimately offered the highest price!

"Haha, you fatso, didn't expect that, did you? I won in the end!"

"Eighteen million for a life, worth it!"

Fat Snake slumped to the side, its eyes devoid of all color.

It was utterly desperate.

Just five hundred thousand Terror Coins short.

Just that little bit more...

"Find a way to transfer the Terror Coins to me."

"Note: Voluntary donation."

"My account is XXX..."

After becoming a challenger, you'll receive a dedicated account.

The rewards after the challenge will be transferred to this account.

"Okay!"

Ah Jin immediately complied.

After a series of operations, eighteen million Terror Coins arrived in the account.

The note "voluntary donation" was added as requested by Lin Bei.

"Let it go."

Lin Bei was honest and kept his word.

With the Terror Coins in the account, he could fulfill his promise.

Yu Mei took the umbrella, untied the ropes binding Ah Jin, and led Ah Jin away from the dungeon.

The hidden door opened and closed, and Ah Jin came into contact with the outside world, smelling fresh air for the first time in a long time, even though the smell was foul and pungent.

"I'm still alive!"

"Great!"

It didn't dare linger there and immediately took off running.

As it quickly ran away, it kept muttering to itself.

“Once we get back, I’ll transfer this money back. With my relationship with the money exchange manager, it’s easy for me. I have plenty of excuses.”

“Also, I want revenge!”

“I’ll kill that wanted criminal!”

“I won’t let that human go either!”

“They all deserve to die!!”

“Prepare for my mad revenge!”

But A-Jin didn’t notice.

Yu Mei, standing in front of the tombstone, suddenly opened her umbrella.

The S-rank Netherworld Artifact, the Death Banquet Oil-Paper Umbrella, was activated for the first time.

Whoosh!

The next moment, a torrential downpour began.

It rained for a kilometer around.

Yu Mei, holding her umbrella, stood in the downpour, her cold gaze piercing through the layers of rain, fixed on A-Jin.

“A massive rainstorm…”

“The Rain Night Butcher’s killing world has arrived.”



Chapter 18 Instantaneous Mystery! Do you like dungeon ramparts?


*Whoosh! Whoosh!*

The rain was torrential, the raindrops almost forming a continuous sheet, pouring down from the sky onto the ground.

Ajin ran wildly through the rain.

It hadn't yet realized the gravity of the situation.

It was fantasizing about revenge.

"Luckily, that human was easy to fool."

"This time, I've hit the jackpot."

"Not only did I survive, but I didn't even have to spend a single Terror Coin."

"I won't let this go unpunished!"

No matter how many Terror Coins it cost, Ajin was determined to avenge itself!

...

On the other side, the challengers patrolling the mass grave were soaked to the bone by the sudden downpour.

"What's going on? This rain just started, in bursts and bursts."

"What bad luck! This kind of downpour can kill a person, and we still have to patrol in the rain. It's practically a rain that accelerates our deaths."

"Ugh!"

"..."

They were just annoyed!

...

Rain Spirit remained standing in place, with no intention of chasing after Ah Jin.

"Perfect timing to try out this umbrella."

"Rain worms..."

"Once inside the rain, they can rapidly multiply, sometimes dozens, even hundreds of times per second. The speed varies depending on the individual."

"Once someone is possessed by a rain worm, the rain will change."

"What will it be like?"

"I'm looking forward to it!"

As she spoke, she lightly tightened her grip on the umbrella, casually swaying the Death Banquet oil-paper umbrella.

The jet-black oil-paper umbrella swayed in the midnight downpour.

It was all black, yet clearly visible, possessing an extremely strong presence.

The next moment, the umbrella surface turned red.

To be precise, it was blood red.

One by one, blood-red insects detached from the umbrella surface and plunged into nearby raindrops.

In a brief instant, the raindrop changed from transparent to blood red.

It stopped falling; gravity seemed to have failed, and the raindrop floated low in the air.

Hundreds of raindrops around it did the same.

The rain insects that had taken over the raindrops began to divide in unison.

The speed was rapid.

The average number of divisions was over sixty.

Hundreds of blood-red raindrops became tens of thousands.

Then, they continued to divide, dividing every second.

Tens of thousands, hundreds of thousands, millions, tens of millions of drops.

The torrential rain falling within a kilometer radius was continuously turning blood red.

The scene was breathtaking.

It began to spread from the rain demons as the center.

All the transparent rain changed color.

It was as if hundreds of drops of deadly venom had been poured into a pool of water, then began to spread rapidly, changing the color of the entire pool.

Moreover, the venom's toxicity didn't dilute as it spread; instead, it grew stronger.

A rain of blood!

Rain Demon stared at the dense crimson surrounding her, her eyes filled with undisguised shock.

"No wonder it's an S-rank Netherworld Artifact."

"The effect is truly terrifying!"

"Each drop of blood rain harbors a rain worm."

Even she felt fear.

S-rank Netherworld Artifacts...

Almost none are weak!

...

In less than ten seconds, not a single drop of clear rain remained.

Ah Jin, who had been running frantically, looked around in terror.

"What happened?"

"The rain changed color?"

"Is this rain or blood?"

"Blood rain?"

It didn't understand what was happening, but instinctively sensed something was wrong.

The rain of blood made Ajin uneasy.

It quickened its pace.

But the area covered by the blood rain was too vast; even running at full speed couldn't get it out quickly.

Just then, a voice rang out from a hundred meters behind it, its words clearly audible, unaffected by the rain.

"Little things, bite it to death!"

It naturally recognized the voice of Yu Mei.

After all, so much had happened.

"What's going on?"

"Didn't you say you'd let me go?"

"Now you want to kill me?"

"You lied to the ghosts?!"

The giant ghost Ajin was both angry and afraid.

Angry because Lin Bei and Yu Mei had broken their promise; they had clearly said they would let it go, but then went back on their word and wanted to kill it.

As for fear, it was simple.

It was afraid of dying!

"Don't talk nonsense."

"Where did he lie to you?"

"He only said he would let you leave the dungeon, he never said he would let you leave this place."

"Die!"

At that moment, countless rain insects, having multiplied, launched their attack, each flapping its wings and swarming towards Ajin.

As the rain insects moved,

the entire rain-soaked area descended into chaos.

In the rain-covered area spanning a kilometer, every drop of rain rushed towards Ajin, wanting to land on him and mercilessly devour him.

The closest group of rain insects had already achieved their goal.

In a few breaths, Ajin was devoured beyond recognition.

His entire body was covered in rain insects, densely packed in dozens of layers.

He was completely powerless to resist.

Only a sliver of life remained.

Seeing this, Yu Mei had her answer. She turned around, opened her umbrella, and walked away.

She didn't even give Ajin a second glance.

...

Rain Demon departed, Ah Jin died, but the blood rain continued.

Another group of victims were the challengers.

They trembled in the blood rain...

"Waaah, I want to go home..."

"..."
