Feeling the time was right, Rain Demon summoned back the rain worms.

All the rain worms, whether released from the umbrella or split off from it, turned to dust and settled on the umbrella surface, layering it systematically.

The umbrella surface turned black again.

Even with so many more rain worms, the height and thickness of the umbrella surface remained unchanged.

"It's back?"

"How is it?"

Lin Bei asked with a smile.

Rain Demon stood a few meters behind Lin Bei.

"It's gone."

"It will never come back."

Lin Bei was satisfied.

Now, that terrifying sum of tens of millions of dollars would be completely his.

“Transfer the Terror Coins, with the note ‘voluntary gift’.”

He even felt a little kind-hearted.

A short while later, another 17.5 million Terror Coins arrived in his account, with the note “voluntary gift”.

Lin Bei naturally kept his promise and gave the fat snake its freedom.

Similarly, Yu Mei once again left with the Death Banquet oil-paper umbrella.

A minute later, Yu Mei returned.

Lin Bei was sitting there bored.

The value of those two eerie creatures had been completely squeezed out, and he didn't know what to do.

“So boring.”

But to his surprise, Yu Mei put the Death Banquet oil-paper umbrella aside and walked step by step to Lin Bei.

“How about I help you pass the time?”

Lin Bei was taken aback, not quite understanding Yu Mei's meaning.

“Do you like this environment?”

“It’s not as luxurious as the car, but it has its own unique charm.”

Looking around, Lin Bei understood.

So Yu Mei wanted to compensate him again!



Chapter 19 The Nightmare of the Demon King!


Afterwards,

【Yu Mei】

【Affection Points: 8659】

He was new to the horror world and didn't know much about it.

Nightmare!

Because Yu Mei is intimately linking arms with you.

Miss Nightmare was intently watching Yu Mei.

Yu Mei and Lin Bei were behaving intimately, their faces full of sweet smiles, like a girl in love, clearly genuinely happy.

This was a side of her she had never seen before!!

In her memory, every time she met Yu Mei, Yu Mei always had a blank face, rarely smiling, even with her good friend, she would only lower her guard slightly, never smiling so happily.

When facing other entities, the situation was even worse, her face full of murderous intent, her eyes filled with resentment.

Miss Nightmare was filled with doubt.

But she knew very well that this wasn't a dream; what was happening before her eyes was real.

A storm raged within her, but she maintained a calm facade—a feat Miss Nightmare could easily accomplish.

Her gaze then fell upon Lin Bei, scrutinizing him from head to toe.

Aside from his appearance, he was just an ordinary person.

Therefore, Miss Nightmare was even more confused.

Even she didn't realize that her curiosity about Lin Bei was slowly growing.

He had essentially greeted Miss Nightmare.

Yu Mei was exceptionally generous.

The current situation wasn't suitable.

This scene was witnessed by Miss Nightmare.

"It's already progressed to this point?"



Chapter 20 What? You only met tonight?


Lin Bei sat in the main seat of the private room.

Let you have a nightmare!

Lin Bei currently has no way to deal with this level of ghost.

If he were to encounter one, he'd have a hard time surviving.

Lin Bei let out a long sigh, putting the matter aside and focusing his attention on the table full of dishes.

Some dishes were familiar, things found in the normal world: Squirrel Fish, Spicy Blood Curd, Sweet and Sour Pork, West Lake Vinegar Fish...

The remaining dishes were completely unfamiliar.

Judging from their appearance, they didn't look like food for humans.

A bowl of blue soup was constantly bubbling, like a pot of poison, with something vaguely swimming in it.

A plate of staple food looked like steamed buns, but it seemed to be breathing faintly.

"No wonder it's food from a horror world."

Not far away, in a secluded private room.

Yu Mei sensed the heavy atmosphere.

It seemed something serious had happened.

"Yu Mei, have you and that person progressed to that stage?"

"The way you walk makes it very obvious..."

"How long have you known each other?"

"Why have I never heard you mention him before?"

She had many questions in her mind.

She was certain.

Yu Mei had never mentioned anything related to this before.

It all happened so suddenly.

And Yu Mei didn't intend to hide it.

“That's right, we've reached the final stage.”

“As for how long you've known him?”

“It's a little embarrassing to say.”

At this moment, Miss Nightmare froze on the spot.

The truth astonished her!!

They'd only known each other for a few hours, and things had progressed to this point?

They were inseparable…

They'd done everything they could…

Are they serious?!

Disbelief filled Miss Nightmare's delicate face.

She knew Yu Mei.

She knew Yu Mei wasn't an impulsive devil.

But now she had done something so utterly outrageous!!

Once again, she began to doubt…

Was she really not dreaming?

Only in dreams could such absurd things happen, right?

“Little…Little Mei, you’re…joking, right?”

“A few hours?”

“Don’t be ridiculous!”

“It’s been at least a few months, hasn’t it?”

Miss Nightmare truly couldn’t believe it.

She had woven countless dreams.

But none of them were this absurd…

Rain Mei shook her head very seriously.

“I’m not joking!”

“I really have only known him for a few hours!”



Chapter 21 further diminishes affection; my relationship with him is purely platonic.


Upon receiving Yu Mei's affirmative answer again, Miss Nightmare completely lost her composure!

"Are you crazy?!"

"We've only known each other for a few hours, and things have progressed to this point?"

"Yu Mei, you're the Rain Night Butcher, how could you do this? Where's your reason? How could you do such a thing?"

Miss Nightmare sensed something was wrong and suspected the problem lay with Lin Bei.

The Yu Mei she knew was ruthless, like a demon, immersed in bloody slaughter, treating piercing screams as melodious music.

Completely different from now!

She couldn't bear to hear anything bad about Lin Bei.

As if controlled by some mysterious force.

Something's wrong!

Very wrong!

Yu Mei truly loves Lin Bei to the point of obsession.

Nearly ten thousand points of goodwill is no joke.

"She's really gone mad!"

Miss Nightmare murmured softly.

She didn't believe in such things.

She had never even considered having love.

In this bizarre world, only power mattered.

After that, Miss Nightmare said nothing more.

Anyway, nothing she did would make a difference.

Rain Demon was too deeply poisoned; words couldn't change her mind.

Meanwhile, Miss Nightmare developed an even stronger interest in Lin Bei, becoming even more convinced that he was no ordinary person and must possess some special abilities.

"Interesting."

“He must have done something.”

“I've heard that some humans are skilled at raising Gu (a type of supernatural insect), he's very likely one of them, and he put a love-related Gu on Yu Mei.”

She made a plan.

She stopped thinking about making Yu Mei wake up immediately.

Instead, she planned to take it slowly, first figuring out Lin Bei's situation, and then finding a way to bring Yu Mei back to normal.

“Little Mei, when you wake up, you'll thank me very much.”

Miss Nightmare made another decision at the same time.

After Yu Mei returns to normal, she'll find an opportunity to get rid of Lin Bei.

To turn an S-level wanted criminal into a hopelessly infatuated lover…

Too dangerous!!

She didn't want to be unfortunately affected too…

and become a love-struck, love-obsessed person.

The door to the private room was pushed open, and Yu Mei walked in with a grin, heading straight for Lin Bei. She sat down beside him, hooking her arm around his, seriously disrupting his meal.

Miss Nightmare stood in the doorway, watching Lin Bei, her beautiful eyes filled with complex emotions—curiosity, wariness, and hostility.

【Endless Nightmare's Mystery—Miss Nightmare】

She is wary of you and harbors murderous intent towards you.

You must find a way to restore Yu Mei to normal.

When that day comes, it will be your death!

Meng Po Soup?

Invented by Meng Po?

Meng Po actually exists in a horror world?



Chapter 22 The system levels up again, with a level four, incredibly powerful function.


Lin Bei quickly accepted the result.

Even encountering judges like Yama (the King of Hell) wouldn't surprise him too much.

After all, this is the world of horror!

A world that is both incredibly absurd and incredibly bizarre.

The food in front of him, besides a few common dishes, consisted of dishes that were increasingly strange.

An eyeball feast, Meng Po's soup, a steamed bun that seemed to breathe…

She introduced each dish.

Therefore, he ate them.

He ate a lot and was very full.

She had already taken some action.

She sent several cunning hands to investigate Lin Bei.

Even if Lin Bei slept all the next few days, his final score would still be very high.

[System Level: lv2..]

[Experience Points: 10000/10000]

[New features unlocked after leveling up.]

[Do you wish to level up?]

Level up?

[Experience Points: 0/100000]

[Upon reaching lv4, the attribute point allocation function will be unlocked.]

This time, the leveling difficulty has increased tenfold.

To reach the next level, a full 100,000 experience points are needed.

Lin Bei didn't immediately use the portable space function, which was easy to understand.

Anyway, the name itself told him what it was.

"Attribute point allocation?"

"Sounds pretty powerful!"

The system helpfully provided an explanation.

【It's a function that can improve the host's various abilities. The host can allocate points to vitality, mental strength, power, speed, physical strength, or a certain ability. Each point allocated will result in a significant improvement.】

【However, points cannot be allocated indefinitely!】

[Attribute points can only be allocated after they have been acquired.]

[The method for acquiring attribute points is simple: it depends on the favorability rating of the target selected by the host. One point is gained when favorability reaches 100, one point when favorability reaches 1000, one point when favorability reaches 10,000, and one point when favorability reaches 100,000.]

[And so on.]

[Theoretically, a lot of attribute points can be gained in a day.]

After listening, Lin Bei understood how it worked.

He had to admit, this function was very powerful.

If used properly, his strength could be greatly improved.

"I need to upgrade the system to level four as soon as possible."

"Attribute point allocation function!"

"Just thinking about it is awesome!"



Chapter 23 A Great Dream, The First Mysterious God


Lin Bei eagerly awaited the moment his system reached level four.

"Level five to six would be ten million!"

"The higher you go, the greater the difficulty of upgrading, and the higher the experience points required."

Lin Bei could accept this situation.

After all, the system's strength increases significantly with each level up.

Her love and loyalty can withstand any test, no matter how difficult.

Never doubt her love and loyalty to you!

[Having a high enough favorability point count for a strange creature isn't a bad thing; excess favorability points can be used for many things.]

[It is recommended that the host work harder to unlock this function as soon as possible!]

Lin Bei raised an eyebrow, somewhat surprised.

But Lin Bei was certain of one thing.

This function was absolutely fantastic!

+1..

Yu Mei's residence was in a very remote location; without her guidance, it was practically impossible to find.

Beside him, Yu Mei sighed with some disappointment.

She hadn't expected Lin Bei to genuinely just want to rest.

"Did I go too far?"

...

While Lin Bei slept soundly, a beautiful dream unfolded.

He was, of course, the protagonist.

In the dream, Lin Bei, dressed in magnificent black robes, sat atop a frozen, dust-covered throne of bones, his right hand propping up his chin as he gazed ahead, his eyes filled with indifference, his face seemingly embodying invincibility.

Before him stood countless eerie beings.

Each elf looked at Lin Bei with a strange gaze.

There was fervor, adoration, trust, appreciation, excitement, longing, and respect...

At the same time, these elves were also quite unusual.

Lin Bei looked around.

He only recognized three elves: Rain Demon, Traffic Light, and Nightmare Lady.

The other elves were all unfamiliar.

But they all looked incredibly powerful!

Some had bull heads, others horse faces.

There is an eerie sight of someone holding a pen in their left hand and a book in their right.

There were eerie creatures, their faces obscured by veils, yet sitting before a pot of soup.

There were also eerie creatures, imposing and radiating power and majesty.

A creature with three eyes, wings on its back, and golden hair.

A creature dressed in a black Lolita dress and wearing a black top hat.

A creature carrying a small flower basket, surrounded by fields of spider lilies.

Two identical twin eerie creatures, one dressed and with white hair, the other entirely black.

These were just a few.

The eerie creatures here weren't just humanoid.

There were also many bizarre beasts.

A canine beast with three heads.

A gigantic octopus beast.

A spider beast over ten meters tall with legs like swords.

There were simply too many to count.

In the dream, there was also the sun and the moon, and stars adorned the dreamscape.

Even as he watched the dream unfold from his deep sleep, Lin Bei felt a chill run down his spine.

Even though he was the one sitting on the throne!

Below, countless eerie beings silently watched Lin Bei, time ticking away second by second.

After an unknown amount of time, Lin Bei, seated on the throne, suddenly stood up.

Then, he walked forward step by step.

Ahead lay only darkness, its end unknown.

The next moment, all the eerie beings and monsters below knelt down, clearly bidding Lin Bei farewell.

The dream ended there, and Lin Bei awoke with a start, finding himself drenched in a cold sweat.

"This dream was suffocating!"

"Who are those monsters?"

"They all look more terrifying than the last."

It took him a while to recover.

Just then, a whisper came from beside him.

"Come on..."

"Come on again!"

"Only eighteen times? I'm not satisfied yet..."

Lin Bei looked towards the source of the voice.

It was Yu Mei talking in her sleep.

He didn't know how to respond.

"Your beautiful dream..."

"It's really exciting!"

...

Outside, the sun rose; even the eerie world had day and night.

Unlike the common settings in movies, the supernatural wasn't affected by daytime.

On the contrary, some special supernatural entities became stronger at dawn.

Lin Bei walked to the window and looked up at the sky.

The sun in the eerie world was equally eerie.

It was a dark purple!

The light it emitted was blinding.

The dark purple sunlight barely illuminated the eerie world, but many things were affected by the light, making it difficult to see their original colors.

"A strange sun."

Lin Bei squinted, trying to look directly at the sun.

【The Dark Sun That Slays Heaven】

【Affection Points: 0】

Chapter 24 Who is the Prey? It's Raining


Chapter Twenty-Four: Who is the Prey? It's Raining

Hiss~

Lin Bei felt a chill run down his spine, a cold tingle at the back of his head.

"A Ghost God-level Anomalous Being..."

"Only the second day, and I've already encountered a Ghost God!!"

"Few Anomalous Beings probably know that the sun hanging in the sky is actually a Ghost God-level being that has reached the pinnacle of power!"

"Fortunately, it won't notice me."

Not being noticed was the most fortunate thing for him.

If he were to attract the attention of this Ghost God now, he would probably turn to ash in an instant.

It's the sun!

Reason kept telling Lin Bei that he couldn't stare directly at this Ghost God for long.

So, he decisively withdrew his gaze and looked at the street shrouded in dark purple light.

The street was deserted, eerily silent.

A luxury car worth millions was parked quietly below.

Lin Bei stood by the window, pondering something.

“I didn’t look up at the moon yesterday.”

“Since the sun is a strange god, could the moon be too?”

“I need to look at the moon tonight.”

He wanted to verify this guess.

Afterwards, Lin Bei began to recall the dream.

Even after waking up, he still remembered it clearly.

In that beautiful dream, there was a sun and a moon.

A sun radiating a dark purple light and a moon radiating a blood-red light.

And many strange things he had never seen before.

“Perhaps I will see them one by one later.”

“What if this beautiful dream can come true?”

He didn’t think too much about it.

Because even if it could come true, it would be a long time from now. The important thing now was to improve his strength and system level.

Otherwise, even if the strange things in the dream appeared, he wouldn’t be able to deal with them.

...

“You were awake a long time ago?”

Lin Bei stood by the window for a long time until he heard Yu Mei’s voice behind him, then he turned his gaze away from the empty view outside the window.

Yu Mei immediately asked again:

"Were you satisfied with your dream?"

Lin Bei nodded.

He was indeed quite satisfied with the dream, even though it didn't have much substantial content.

Eighteen times?

Goodbye, goodbye!

Okay, okay.

Two more times.

Lin Bei's safety was at stake; she wouldn't joke about it.

Night fell quickly.

If Lin Bei said he was short, then he was!

A few minutes later, the ultra-luxury taxi pulled up outside a small alley.

Lin Bei deliberately circled the area a few times to make sure there were no strange occurrences.

“This will do.”

He stood alone in the alley, took out a cigarette, lit it, and a small flame ignited in the darkness.

Yu Mei sat in the off-road car.

She had put on her black raincoat again.

It blended perfectly into the night.

Without pressing your head against the glass, it was impossible to see inside the car.

Lin Bei leaned against the wall, looking up at the starlit night sky.

Luckily, there was a moon tonight!

He stared intently at the moon.

The moonlight was blood red, radiating a cold, crimson glow that stained the surrounding night sky crimson.

Just as Lin Bei had expected, an information panel appeared at that moment.

In its eyes, you are just one of countless people basking in the moonlight.

A dozen minutes later, the chainsaw-wielding maniac, carrying a chainsaw on its shoulder, successfully arrived at Lin Bei's location, guided by the soul mark.

Upon arriving, it saw the ultra-luxury taxi, worth millions.

The car was parked by the roadside.

The engine was off, the lights were off, and it was completely dark inside; nothing could be seen.

"The person isn't in the car?"

The chainsaw-wielding maniac looked around, quickly locking onto the alleyway, and saw Lin Bei standing against the wall, lighting a cigarette; the small flame was particularly conspicuous.

It stroked the chainsaw in its hand.

"Found him."

"Don't rush."

"Kill him immediately."

With that, the chainsaw-wielding maniac walked step by step towards Lin Bei, a ferocious smile on its face.

The closer it got to Lin Bei, the more ferocious its smile became.

It had already assumed Lin Bei was dead.

Hearing the commotion, Lin Bei tossed away his cigarette; the tedious wait was finally over.

He smiled at the Chainsaw Maniac.

“My 500,000!”

“You’ve finally arrived!”

“Welcome, welcome!”

The Chainsaw Maniac realized something was wrong and stopped moving forward.

Because Lin Bei’s demeanor clearly indicated he was prepared and showed no surprise.

“500,000?”

“What does he mean?”

“Is he referring to my bounty?”

That was the only connection it could make with 500,000.

It found it laughable.

An ordinary person, so arrogant?

The Chainsaw Maniac was absolutely certain that Lin Bei was just an ordinary human, unable to even defeat the weakest of the monsters, let alone deal with a monster of its level.

Thinking this, its unease dissipated.

"My bounty is indeed 500,000."

"Want it?"

"You'll have to be alive to get it!"

Despite saying this, the chainsaw maniac didn't make a move.

It observed its surroundings, trying to eliminate any danger.

"No danger."

"But thinking about it carefully, it's actually quite strange."

"An ordinary person driving a car that expensive?"

"That in itself is a huge suspicious point."

"Could this be a conspiracy against me?"

If it were just the fact that Lin Bei was driving a luxury car, the chainsaw maniac wouldn't have thought too much about it.

But Lin Bei's reaction today was really off.

Too calm, too agitated.

Completely unlike a dying prey.

In fact, quite the opposite.

Compared to a prey, Lin Bei seemed more like a hunter.

Just then, Lin Bei's voice rang out again.

“You don’t need to worry about that.”

“I’m taking your head.”

“If I couldn’t do it, why would I be waiting here?”

The instant his words fell,

rain began to fall.

It’s raining!



Chapter 25 Grade A Netherworld Artifact, Berserker Blood-Shattering Bone Saw


At the entrance to the alley, a sinister figure dressed in a black raincoat, holding a black oil-paper umbrella, stood there, his cold gaze fixed ahead.

It was none other than Rain Demon.

In her line of sight, the chainsaw maniac, carrying a chainsaw, stood cautiously, his entire body tense, clearly afraid to move rashly.

“Is that it?”

“What’s with that chainsaw?”

“I’ll kill you in a minute!”

Further ahead, Lin Bei stood with his hands in his pockets, letting the rain fall on him.

The rain was heavy, pouring down, the air icy cold.

The sound of the rain was also loud, drowning out most other sounds.

Judging by the intensity of the rain, it wouldn’t be long before this alley was flooded, the ground collapsed, the walls crumbled.

And a rainy night was perfect for slaughter.

…

The chainsaw maniac’s brows furrowed even more deeply.

This rain was too sudden, too deliberate, too unpredictable.

It started raining without warning!

One second the sky was clear and the moon was bright, the next a torrential downpour began.

The rain lashed against the chainsaw, sharpening the blade considerably.

The rain was so heavy that it could barely see Lin Bei.

"I have to be careful."

"He might take advantage of the rain to attack me."

But the chainsaw maniac was disappointed.

Lin Bei didn't move at all.

He just stood there in the heavy rain.

But it didn't know.

Even the rain was gentle on Lin Bei.

Lin Bei felt no discomfort from the rain.

Every drop of rain was gently caressing him.

But the rain that fell on the chainsaw maniac was violent; every drop caused it pain.

"Why isn't he moving?"

After waiting for half a minute, the chainsaw maniac still hadn't seen Lin Bei make a move.

It didn't know what to do.

The rain intensified, and the situation worsened.

"No."

"I can't just wait around."

"I have to attack!"

With that thought, the chainsaw maniac decisively started its chainsaw.

Whoosh!

The saw blade spun rapidly, a dangerous glint in its eyes. The chainsaw maniac walked forward into the downpour, a ferocious yet cautious smile on its face.

"Human, you're going to die!"

"In a moment, my chainsaw will saw right off your head, cleanly slicing you in two!"

"One half will be for drinking, the other half will nourish my chainsaw."

The moment it started its chainsaw, its reason vanished, its caution diminished infinitely.

In its place came arrogance and hubris.

Lin Bei witnessed the entire transformation of the chainsaw maniac and made a rough guess in his mind.

"Will this chainsaw affect the user's sanity?"

"It's clearly gone berserk!"

"Looks like a necromancer with certain side effects. I'll have to be careful when using it."

He wouldn't abandon a powerful necromancer just because it might have side effects.

The side effects weren't the necromancer's problem.

But if he was affected by the necromancer's side effects, then it was definitely the user's problem.

...

Just as the chainsaw maniac took his first step, Rain Demon's voice rang out amidst the torrential rain, completely unaffected by the sound, incredibly clear, as if shouting right next to his ear.

"Take another step forward, and I'll take your life."

At that moment, the chainsaw maniac experienced what it meant to be utterly terrified.

A sharp intuition told it that the freak who spoke those words wasn't joking.

If it went any further, it would truly die instantly.

Lost reason returned in an instant, and the forgotten fear returned.

It stopped.

Getting soaked in the rain, it turned around and looked at the end of the rain curtain.

There...

It was Rain Demon!!

Coincidentally, a bolt of lightning flashed.

The darkness was briefly illuminated.

The chainsaw maniac clearly saw the figure at the end.

"It's her!!"

"S-Class Wanted Freak, nicknamed Rain Night Butcher."

"Distinguishing features include wearing a black raincoat, preferring to go out late at night, possessing abilities related to rain, and this is Gray Abyss City."

"She's helping this human taxi driver?"

The chainsaw maniac felt something was very wrong with this situation.

It had heard other information about Rain Demon during its hunts.

Among the information was information related to Rain Demon's target selection.

"It's said she only targets wealthy monsters or rich people."

"But now she's helping a rich person?!"

"She doesn't seem like someone who hates the rich at all. She threatened to kill me right off the bat..."

"What Rain Night Butcher? She's clearly a lackey."

"This information..."

"It's ridiculously fake!"

"What bad luck!"

The Chainsaw Maniac couldn't help but curse under its breath.

The information and the actual situation were completely inconsistent.

But something felt off.

Even if the information was wrong, how could it be this bad?

Suppressing his fear, the chainsaw maniac asked Yu Mei:

"The Rain Night Butcher?"

"What do you want?"

"To protect this human whose net worth is at least tens of millions?"

"This is different from the you I know."

"I heard you're extremely resentful of the rich, specifically targeting wealthy people."

"But you..."

It stopped mid-sentence, making Yu Mei impatient.

"Shut up."

"What I do is none of your business."

"That's right, I resent the rich."

"But he's an exception!"

"Even if he only has tens of millions, even if he has hundreds of millions, or even billions, I wouldn't hate him, much less harm him."

"I want to protect him, regardless of his wealth, simply because he is who he is!"

Yu Mei wanted to protect Lin Bei as a person.

Nothing else mattered.

Even if Lin Bei was ugly, fat, and useless, she wouldn't despise him.

The chainsaw maniac looked astonished.

The answer was unexpected.

An S-class wanted criminal, constantly talking about protecting someone?

And protecting a human?

Are you crazy?!

What has that human done to deserve this?!

It wanted to say something, but Rain Demon wouldn't give it a chance.

"Die."

The cold voice fell, and the dense, wall-like rain erupted into a raging storm.

Countless rain insects detached from the Death Banquet's oil-paper umbrella, rushing into the large raindrops.

The rain turned blood red once more.

It relentlessly poured into the chainsaw maniac's body.

It experienced the agony of being devoured by countless insects.

A scream instantly rang out.

Successfully drowning out the sound of the torrential rain.

"No!"

"It hurts!"

"Help me!!"

"Why...why..."

"Why would you help a human like this?"

But soon, the torrential rain drowned everything, including life and hope.

A minute later, the crimson rain subsided, leaving everything back to normal except for the lingering dampness.

The chainsaw maniac had been completely devoured by the rain worms, leaving only his head—enough to collect the bounty.

And the chainsaw, now covered in bits of flesh and stained with blood.

"This chainsaw is mine."

Lin Bei walked to the chainsaw and casually touched it.

Unexpectedly, an information panel appeared.

【Berserk Blood Bone-Shattering Saw】

[Grade: Grade A Funerary Object]

【

A very sharp chainsaw.

It can cut and slash.

A true weapon of slaughter.

When using this chainsaw, every blood and cell in your body will enter a berserk state, with anger and excitement rapidly eroding your reason.

This state will only end when the chainsaw stops or you suffer a major blow.

This chainsaw has no fancy abilities.

Its greatest strength is its destructive power!

Anything in its path will be cut into pieces, pulverized into dust.

The host's current strength is relatively low; please use this weapon with caution.

Improper use will have serious side effects.

】



Chapter 26 The Underworld Office: Recruiting Temporary Human Workers


"Not bad, an A-grade artifact."

Lin Bei planned to use this chainsaw himself.

After all, the information panel clearly stated that.

The strongest point of the Berserker Blood Bone-Shattering Saw is its destructive power, completely different from the complex abilities possessed by the Death Banquet Oil-Paper Umbrella.

Perfect for him.

He's currently just an ordinary person, and this kind of artifact with powerful destructive capabilities is most suitable for him.

"Crisis over."

That’s why she planned to do this.

Yu Mei didn't linger either.

Wearing a raincoat and carrying an umbrella on her back, she quickly blended into the dark night.

“With this umbrella, my strength has increased to another level.”

“I can choose some stronger targets to target.”

Chapter 27: Bounty in Hand, Forced to Become a Temporary Worker

Yu Mei, on the other hand, was an S-rank wanted creature because she had a clear objective, specifically targeting wealthy individuals.

Chapter 28 Ms. Moon Oriole's Misotis, an Egg

Because he acquired the god-level skill of "Master Driver," the entire map of Gray Abyss City was etched into Lin Bei's mind.

After driving for more than ten minutes, Lin Bei arrived at an abandoned factory.

Buzz!!

"An A-grade Netherworld artifact focused on destructive power, and it's so difficult to cut it. These three suitcases are probably quite good Netherworld artifacts."

"The things packed in these suitcases..."

"Definitely good!!"

Lin Bei's anticipation grew even stronger.

"Are you crazy..."

The contents inside were equally strange.

Various stones and fruits.

None of it looked worth much.

"I give up!"

"Another suitcase full of junk."

At this point, Lin Bei could only pin his hopes on the third suitcase.

He looked at the third suitcase.

And inside was an egg!

The egg was black.

Dark red flame patterns grew on it.

They were almost invisible unless you looked closely.

"An egg?"

"What kind of egg is this?"

Lin Bei couldn't figure it out.

He could only barely confirm that the egg was related to fire.

Just then, an information panel appeared before him.

【Nether Phoenix (Unhatched)】

【Affection Points: Undetectable (Incomplete Life Form)】

【Relationship: None】

【Level: None】

【

A living Nether Phoenix egg.

Inside lies a legendary Nether Phoenix.

You can try to hatch this egg.

You might have a chance to obtain a Nether Phoenix!

】

Lin Bei's eyes widened instantly!

“What?”

“This is a Nether Phoenix egg?!”

“This!!!”

“I’m stunned, buddy!!”

His previous disappointment vanished instantly; he was completely dumbfounded!

He never expected that the third suitcase would contain a Nether Phoenix egg.

What is a Nether Phoenix?

Lin Bei didn’t know.

But he did know.

Since it was related to a phoenix, it couldn’t possibly be bad.

Besides, if the Nether Phoenix wasn't strong enough, why would a place like the Nether Phoenix Residence exist?

Without any hesitation, he decisively packed the three suitcases into his personal space.

"Hide them first; these things can't be exposed."

"Otherwise, they'll bring me to my death."

"Nether Phoenix Residence, Nether Phoenix Egg..."

"It's certain that this Nether Phoenix Egg is extremely important to the Nether Phoenix Residence. No wonder the owner's three children went to retrieve it themselves."

"However, judging from the strange behavior of those three creatures, they clearly don't know what's in the suitcases..."

"This is a bargain for me!"



Chapter 29: The system reaches level four, unlocking attribute point allocation.


At this moment, Lin Bei's desire to hatch the Nether Phoenix egg was exceptionally strong.

Please!

This is a Nether Phoenix egg!

Inside the egg is a phoenix!!

Although it's a phoenix that sounds a little eerie...

But he knew he couldn't do it.

"I have to be patient."

"Hatching a Nether Phoenix isn't easy; it can't be done in a few days."

"I need to be well-prepared."

"Losing a Nether Phoenix egg will definitely enrage the owner of the Nether Phoenix Residence, who will use every means to find it."

There was no other way; Lin Bei could only endure it.

After confirming that he hadn't left any trace, he drove away in his ultra-luxury taxi.

Lin Bei planned to pretend he knew nothing and continue as a taxi driver to complete the first round of challenges.

Only by passing the first round of challenges could he buy items in the Thriller Shop and obtain the corresponding rewards.

Therefore, the best course of action right now was to survive until the end of this round of challenges.

...

As the vehicle moved forward, Lin Bei continued communicating with the system.

“By the way, System, does the personal space have a shielding function?”

“Won’t it be located by some kind of force?”

This was his biggest worry.

If the owner of Mingfeng Residence had left such a contingency plan, wouldn’t he be in a very dangerous situation?

【Host, please rest assured!】

【The personal space is an independently created world.】

【Only the host can access it.】

Lin Bei breathed a sigh of relief.

At least keeping the Mingfeng Egg in the personal space was safe.

...

Night fell again in the blink of an eye.

Lin Bei returned to the taxi company.

Originally, there were double-digit participants in this round of challenges.

But now, besides him, only six people remained.

Only two of them were unharmed; the remaining four were injured to some extent.

One person lost an eye, one lost two fingers...

And one had a bandage wrapped around their left hand.

The last person was covered in scratches.

The two unharmed were none other than the two more experienced challengers.

Although they weren't injured, their expressions were grim, clearly indicating they had endured considerable suffering.

Lin Bei, who had just gotten off the vehicle, was taken aback by the sight.

"What happened?"

"Why are they all in such a sorry state?"

"Is this job really that difficult?"

He didn't feel any difficulty in this round of challenges at all.

Were the passengers difficult?

Would the passengers harm the driver?

Would other hearses harass him on the road?

Sorry...

None of that happened.

On the contrary, Lin Bei had been having a very comfortable time these past few days.

Not only was he being served delicious food and drinks by a beautiful and mysterious woman, but he had also easily earned tens of millions of Terror Coins.

He even began to wonder...

Was this round of challenges a freebie?

Meanwhile, the six surviving challengers were also watching Lin Bei.

Lin Bei stood in front of the supercar, looking radiant and full of energy, a blissful smile spreading across his face, his lips unconsciously curving upwards, his fingers tapping rhythmically.

He looked like he'd been a groom for several nights and suddenly become rich!

Completely different from their disheveled appearance...

It was utterly incongruous!!

Therefore, the six of them felt a deep resentment.

They were filled with envy and resentment, feeling it was incredibly unfair, yet each of them wanted to replace Lin Bei.

"Damn it, you can tell he's been having a blast these past few days, acting like a spoiled rich kid!"

"Why? We're all challengers, but why does he get all the privileges? He's enjoying life while we're all struggling to survive, it's infuriating!!"

"I'm so jealous. He probably earns more in just an hour of driving than we do in seven days."

"Heh, disgusting nepotism."

"..."

...

Lin Bei stayed at the taxi company for a while.

But the traffic light didn't change.

"Still not back?"

"Then come back tomorrow."

He drove away.

The next few days are just a matter of getting through them peacefully.

This round of the professional challenge will be easy to pass.

...

Several hours passed.

The system notification suddenly sounded.

【System experience points have reached their limit; further increases are impossible.】

【Rain Fairy's favorability has reached its current limit. Further increases can be made after the system upgrades.】

【System Level: lv3..】

【Affected Areas: Those below the Ancient Realm】

【Do you wish to level up?】

【If you confirm leveling up, the current target's favorability increase will be terminated.】

Lin Bei was driving.

He didn't hesitate for a second.

“Level up.”

His experience points were already full; what was the point of keeping them?

Lin Bei continued driving.

“System level four.”

“The experience points needed to reach level five are a terrifying number.”

“Take it slow…”

A few seconds later, the system notification sounded again.

【Ding!】

【System level has been upgraded to lv4!】

【System level: lv4..】

【Scope of effect: Evil Spirits and below】

【System functions: Favorability increase, Lucky Roulette, Portable space, Attribute points…】

【Experience points: 0/1,000,000】

【Upon reaching lv5, the function of the portal will be unlocked.】

Just as Lin Bei had predicted.

The required experience points had increased tenfold again!

From 100,000 to 1 million.

His expression was anything but pleasant.

A million experience points wasn't too difficult to obtain; it would only take a dozen days.

But what about later? Ten million experience points, one hundred million experience points, one billion experience points?

Gaining so many experience points isn't an easy feat.

【Host doesn't need to worry too much about this.】

【There will naturally be ways to accelerate experience point acquisition later.】

Hearing this, Lin Bei breathed a sigh of relief.

This was exactly what he wanted to hear!

“That's good.”

Afterwards, Lin Bei focused his attention on the unlockable feature called the Teleportation Gate.

“Teleportation Gate?”

“What kind of feature is that?”

“Let me explain briefly.”

【The Teleportation Gate function isn't simply about creating a gate at two different points. Instead, it allows you to create a gate on a monster whose favorability rating has surpassed 100,000. Once activated, it instantly teleports you to the monster's location, and vice versa.】

【There are no limits on the number or distance of teleportation gates.】

Lin Bei understood.

“A very convenient function.”

“After activating this function, I can find Yu Mei anytime.”

“If I encounter any serious trouble later, I can bring over all the strange entities that have established teleportation portals.”

“Excellent!”

He had already figured out the correct use of this function.

Hoo~

At the most crucial moment, Lin Bei took a deep breath, his eyes filled with barely concealed excitement.

“System…”

“Can I allocate attribute points now?”

【Not at the moment.】

【Host's current attribute points are 0…】

【It is recommended that the host find a way to acquire attribute points!】

…



Chapter 30: Ten consecutive draws on the Golden Roulette - Challenge ends early?

[Sorry!]

Although frustrated, he had no choice but to try and acquire attribute points.

Fortunately, acquiring attribute points was relatively simple.

Half a minute later, Lin Bei made a decision, parking the car haphazardly on the side of the road.

He sat in the car, watching the occasional strange creature passing by ahead.

There weren't many strange creatures.

Lin Bei nodded decisively, without the slightest hesitation.

Favorability Points...

Currently, their only use is spinning the lucky roulette.

He clearly remembered the details of the Golden Roulette.

No gray or white orbs meant he wouldn't get a normal reward.

Blue and green orbs made up half the deck, purple orbs were plentiful, and gold and red orbs were also plentiful; the probability of getting something good wasn't low.

A pure gold roulette appeared in Lin Bei's mind.

The orbs of various colors were randomly arranged, with no discernible pattern.

The pointer, also made of gold, began to slowly rotate.

At first, slowly.

But faster and faster.

A few seconds passed, leaving only afterimages.

Soon, the golden pointer stopped.

It stopped on a purple area.

A rare reward!

Lin Bei's eyes widened immediately, filled with surprise and astonishment.

"A rare item on the first draw?"

"Really lucky!"

The system notification sounded at that moment:

[Obtained one bottle of Superhuman Potion!]

Unfortunately, his luck didn't last, stopping on a green square.

A good reward...

[Received one case of Energy Recovery Drinks.]

Third time, blue square, general reward.

[Received one Tetanus Dagger.]

Fourth, fifth, and sixth times, all general rewards.

A bunch of junk.

On the seventh time, the golden pointer stopped in the purple area again.

The second rare reward!!

[Received High-Quality Strength-Boosting Gloves with Strong Anti-Slip Design]

The eighth and ninth times, more junk.

Finally...

The tenth time, the last time!!

The guaranteed reward!

Lin Bei looked at the golden roulette wheel with anticipation.

Spinning and spinning...

Finally, it stopped on a golden ball of light!

A divine reward!!

Lin Bei almost cried out!

*Whoosh*

His breathing was out of control, several times more rapid than a few seconds ago. His heartbeat and speed had noticeably changed—a common sign of extreme emotional excitement.

"Awesome!"

[Gained Divine Skill - Mind Recovery!!]

[Divine Skill fusion complete...]

[With this skill, your mind will exist forever!]

[Only in special circumstances...]

[For example, when reason is about to succumb to madness, when the mind is about to collapse, or when intense grief severely affects the spirit.]

[Whenever this happens, when you're in a particularly bad state, your mind will begin to fully recover, allowing you to regain calm and be unaffected by anything. It will completely suppress your fear, allowing you to face even the most terrifying beings without fear. It will transform your negative emotions into power and unleash your potential.]

[It's possible to awaken some kind of power.]

[It's possible your physical abilities will greatly improve.]

[There are many other possibilities.]

[Always believe in the power of your mind!]

Compared to the masterful "Expert Driver" technique,

Although the divine technique fusion was complete, Lin Bei felt nothing, as if nothing had happened.

But he knew very well.

The "Mind Recovery" divine technique was far more powerful than the "Expert Driver" technique!!

"In other words, this skill can't be used unless this situation occurs."

"So it's a passive skill."

"Well, it's alright. Considering how powerful this skill is, I won't complain."

Overall, Lin Bei was extremely satisfied with the result of this ten-pull of the Golden Roulette.

He still had over 40,000 favorability points remaining.

He wanted to do another ten-pull of the Golden Roulette.

But Lin Bei kept telling himself not to be impulsive.

"Hold back, you must hold back."

+1...

+1...

+1...

More and more innocent passing strange creatures had their favorability increased to one hundred.

Regardless of what they originally intended to do, the moment their favorability reached one hundred, they arrived near the ultra-luxury taxi.

No, it should be said, they arrived beside Lin Bei.

They surrounded him in concentric circles.

There were hundreds of these eerie creatures.

They were chattering incessantly, trying to communicate with Lin Bei.

This gave Lin Bei a headache.

The road was completely blocked.

Cars behind couldn't pass, and many bewildered creatures stopped to watch.

Lin Bei felt like a celebrity.

Something was wrong.

Lin Bei belatedly realized what was happening and didn't know what to say.

"Seriously?"

"What are you all doing here?"

"You're blocking my view!!"

But there were too many creatures, and the situation was too chaotic; his words were drowned out.

...

In a hidden corner of darkness outside the swarm of creatures, one creature was watching the lively scene.

It...

was the Hunter of this round of the professional challenge!!

“We can’t let him keep messing around like this.”

“What he’s doing is far beyond the scope of this round of the professional challenge, yet he hasn’t broken any rules.”

“He is indeed working as a taxi driver…”

“It’s just that he drives a good car, and he completed several days’ worth of performance requirements in one go. There are just a lot of strange and inexplicable favors done to him, but I really can’t find anything wrong with him.”

“But I have the authority to end this round of the challenge early.”

“He needs to get out of here as soon as possible; I don’t want him ruining the business I’m responsible for.”



Chapter 31 Time Stops, the Regulator's Annoyance!


The supervisor's responsibilities are complex.

Simply put, they are accountable for all professional challenges under their jurisdiction.

For example...

Did the challenger violate any rules?

Did this round of challenges cause any negative impact?

Did anything uncontrollable happen?

In such situations, immediate action is required to minimize trouble.

And the observation and judgment of the challenger.

Every point is important.

At this moment, in the supervisor's eyes, Lin Bei was a huge problem.

He'd only been here a few days and already caused so much trouble...

Should he stay any longer?

Who knows how much more trouble he'd cause!

"It's decided."

"End his challenge early."

"Also, give him an extremely high rating, so his colleagues will pay special attention to him when they encounter him later."

Having made the decision, the supervisor took out a brass pocket watch from his pocket.

He opened the watch's cover.

The next moment, time in this area stopped!

The traffic stopped, the horde of monsters stopped, even the wind and the moonlight seemed to cease.

The Overseer emerged from a hidden corner of darkness, walking step by step towards the million-dollar luxury car surrounded by a multitude of frenzied, bizarre creatures.

With each seemingly ordinary step, it would reappear dozens of meters away, covering a hundred meters in just two steps.

Besides the Overseer, Lin Bei was also unaffected by the time stop.

He noticed the anomaly immediately.

Because it was all too obvious!

The monsters crammed inside the car remained completely still, resembling exquisitely crafted, lifelike wax figures.

The strange creatures surrounding the car were the same.

They huddled together, maintaining bizarre postures.

The music that had been playing had stopped, and the silence inside the car was eerily quiet.

Through a small gap, Lin Bei looked into the distance.

Similarly, every strange creature, every hearse, the lights and screens of the street shops were all frozen in a state of absolute stillness.

"Time stopped?"

"Only I can move?"

"What's going on?"

"Am I being watched?"

Lin Bei frowned instinctively, knowing that some strange creature was definitely causing trouble in the shadows.

But he couldn't pinpoint who it was.

He cautiously surveyed his surroundings.

A few seconds later, Lin Bei made his discovery.

He watched as a mysterious, eerie creature, appearing and disappearing with each step, rapidly approached him from beyond the encirclement.

A related information panel appeared before him.

【Hunter - Volcano】

【Affection Points: -30】

【Relationship: Dissatisfied】

【Level: Cataclysmic】

【

It's a Class C supervisor, responsible for managing five professions, including the taxi driver profession you're currently challenging.

Except for private areas, it has a complete grasp of everything you do in the outside world, including the luxury car's constant shaking.

You've really annoyed it!

It's seen thousands, if not tens of thousands, of challengers, but none have baffled it like you.

It doesn't understand why those weirdos become your sycophants, why they lose their minds the moment they see you...

An unprecedented, unheard-of situation!

While annoyed, it just wants you to leave as soon as possible, worried you'll cause more trouble.

It just wants to quietly achieve its goals!

】

Lin Bei's eyes widened.

The situation was still beyond his expectations.

He initially thought it was some greedy monster attacking him, but unexpectedly, it was the hunter himself who made the move.

"A C-rank hunter..."

"Possessing catastrophic strength, yet only rated C."

"Is this rating based on strength?"

"If that's true, how strong would a B-rank hunter be?"

"What about an A-rank hunter? What's the situation then?"

"And then there's the S-rank, above A-rank..."

Lin Bei didn't dare to think about these things.

The more he thought about it, the more powerless and desperate he felt!

Now, Lin Bei roughly guessed why the hunter, Volcano, was doing this.

"Is he planning to let me leave?"

"What a pity..."

"Haven't even seen the second traffic light yet."

"But finishing the challenge early isn't bad."

Just then, the Hunter, Volcano, weaved through the horde of monsters and forcefully arrived beside Lin Bei.

It gave a polite smile first, then its expression changed instantly, becoming serious and earnest.

"Challenger Lin Bei."

"Now, officially informing you."

"Due to your outstanding performance, you have completed seven days' worth of tasks ahead of schedule, and your earnings have far exceeded the stipulated amount. Therefore, your rookie round challenge is officially over."

"You will then be randomly teleported to a safe zone. The challenge rewards will be calculated after you enter your assigned room."

"Alright, that concludes the notification."

"Until we meet again."

While the Hunter, Volcano, said "until we meet again," it actually wished it would never see Lin Bei again.

After saying that, it turned to leave.

But Lin Bei suddenly interrupted.

"I refuse!"

"I don't want to leave early."

"I haven't experienced this profession enough yet."

As soon as he finished speaking, the information panel reappeared.

【Hunter - Volcano】

【Affection Points: -50】

【Relationship: Dissatisfied + Angry!】

【Level: Disaster】

【

It's furious!

Just because it heard the last thing it wanted to hear.

It just wants you to leave!

The sooner the better!

It doesn't want to see you again!

See you again?

No!

It's goodbye forever!

】

Huff!

Hunter Volcano took a deep breath, suppressing the anger surging within it.

“No.”

“Please follow the rules.”

“Don't worry, you won't miss any of your rewards.”

“Farewell!”

Lin Bei could only sigh deeply.

“Alright, let’s leave it at that for now.”

“This is Gray Abyss City…”

“I’ll come back to this city sooner or later, and then I’ll deal with these strange things again. Let them develop freely for now.”

The instant he finished speaking, everything in his vision blurred. Images were thrown into a shredder, turning into dust at an incredible speed.

Lin Bei’s five senses were also rapidly disappearing…

Until finally, all five senses were gone, and he had no idea of ​​his surroundings.

When he regained his senses, Lin Bei found himself in an unfamiliar place.

The first thing he saw was a spacious hall.

The hall was filled with all sorts of equipment; it could be said to have everything.

A huge TV screen, vending machines stocked with various drinks, a large freezer filled with elaborately packaged meals, and luxuriously comfortable sofas.

There were even basketball hoops, punching bags, and ping-pong tables—it was all-encompassing.

Besides these things, there were people and eeriness.

Information boards kept appearing before my eyes...



Chapter 32 The Twins of Roses, Safe Zone Rules

The administrator would say the same thing every time a new Challenger checked in.

Hmm...

They were really just watching.

Two female eeries stood outside.

Their hair was casually tied up, yet still incredibly eye-catching.

Even in their simple, uniform cleaning uniforms, their impressive figures were undeniable.

They were pushing a cleaning cart.

The cart was filled with various cleaning tools.

Information about the two eeries appeared before Lin Bei's eyes.

[Cleaner - Xiao Bai (True identity: White Rose)]

[Affection Points: 0]

[Level: Evil Eerie (Severely injured, originally Nightmare level)]

[

White Rose, the younger of the Rose Twins...]

Severely injured in a battle, she escaped and successfully disguised herself to work here as a cleaner.

Her daily routine consists of cleaning.

She's simultaneously feigning injury and preparing for future revenge!

Before her injury, she was incredibly strong.

Because she and her sister, Red Rose, caused so much trouble, they earned the nickname "Rose Twins."

Never, ever provoke those sisters!

Even severely injured and with significantly reduced strength, they can still easily kill you!

【Level: Evil Ghost (Severely injured, originally Nightmare level)】

【

Red Rose, the elder of the Rose Twins】

The reason here is the same as above.

Compared to her younger sister, White Rose, her personality is relatively passionate, while White Rose is quite aloof.

She's not someone to be trifled with!

When the two sisters fight together, their combined power is no less than that of a Hell-level being!

】

Lin Beiren was completely dumbfounded!

Huh?

What kind of outrageous situation is this?

These two cleaning ghosts...

Are they hiding such a shocking secret?!

My goodness!

If this got out, what a sensation it would cause!

For a moment, he didn't even know how to face these two sisters.

A suffocating feeling spread rapidly through the elevator.

The pressure was immense!!

Fortunately, the Rose twins only went up one floor.

The elevator stopped on the seventh floor a few seconds later, and the two sisters pushed their cleaning cart out of the elevator.

Only Lin Beiren remained in the elevator.

The elevator continued upwards, stopping again on the fourteenth floor, at which point Lin Bei's demeanor finally returned to normal.

"What an astonishing discovery."

"If I couldn't see the information panel, I would never have noticed the anomaly of these two sisters."

"Are they disguising themselves for revenge?"

"Currently, they're at the level of Evil Ghost..."

"Just within my range of ability to increase their favorability!"

"Should I take both sisters down?"

"The White Rose and the Red Rose..."

"Although their strength is mediocre right now, as long as their injuries are completely healed, they can return to their peak. I'll essentially max out the favorability of two super-powerful female ghosts ahead of time!!"

He took a deep breath.

He already had a clear answer in his mind.

Then, Lin Bei stepped out of the elevator and walked to room 1414.

Each room has the same door, with a bright red sign on the right-hand wall displaying different numbers.

They are equipped with high-tech locks.

There are no keyholes, no fingerprint scanners, not even doorknobs; the door opens automatically whenever the resident wants it open.

Only residents can freely enter and exit; no one else, including the supernatural beings and even the safe zone administrators, is allowed to enter rooms occupied by challengers.

“Open the door.”

Lin Bei whispered.

The door in front of him opened automatically, disappearing into the wall.

He instinctively looked ahead.

The first thing he saw was a picture frame.

But it didn't contain a picture; instead, it contained several lines of text.

The top line read the title:

'Cozy Apartment Rules'

Below, of course, was the content.

Rule 1: Do not harm or insult the working monsters in the safe zone in any way, or you will be attacked by them all.

Rule 2: No credit, no dining and dashing, and no borrowing Terror Coins from working monsters; otherwise, you will have to repay a hundredfold.

Rule 3: Outside of professional challenge time, you may not leave the safe zone without the administrator's permission. Once you leave, you will permanently lose protection. Even if you survive in the dangerous and terrifying world, you will no longer be protected and will be blacklisted by all safe zones.

Rule 4: You can communicate with the working monsters in the safe zone. You can pay them enough Terror Coins to do what you ask. As long as you have enough Terror Coins, they will willingly do anything for you.

Rule 5: No malicious incidents are allowed between residents. If they occur, you will be expelled by the administrator.

Rule 6: Going to the 18th floor and the basement is forbidden. If you do not believe this, you will bear the consequences.

'Rule Seven: No bringing supernatural entities outside the safe zone into the safe zone.'

'Rule Eight: None yet, to be determined later...'

There are only seven rules.

After reading them only once, Lin Bei had memorized them.

He would do his best not to violate them.

The next moment, Lin Bei stepped into the room.

Officially entering his own absolutely safe zone.



Chapter 33 Divine and Demonic Body, Power Surges, Terrifying Shop


The moment Lin Bei entered the room, a deep, emotionless female voice echoed in his mind:

[Congratulations on successfully completing this round of the career challenge!]

[The challenged career was taxi driver, with an overall danger level of B!]

[Rewards are being calculated...]

[Calculation complete...]

[Your performance in this round of the challenge is ultimately rated S!]

[The rewards obtained are as follows...]

[10,000 Terror Coins, seven random vehicle summoning tickets, one official driver's license, one vehicle operation license, one driver-type career advancement challenge card, five repair potions, and five sanity potions.]

Lin Bei was slightly disappointed.

"These are just small rewards."

"But something is better than nothing..."

He wasn't particularly surprised.

After all, he had only completed the beginner round of the challenge, which wasn't very difficult to begin with, so receiving these rewards was perfectly normal.

The rewards for completing more difficult careers later on would naturally be several levels higher.

"The reward this time isn't important."

"The important thing is successfully unlocking the Horror Shop!"

That's what Lin Bei cares about!

The Horror Shop...

It's key for most challengers to improve their strength!!

Just then, that deep female voice sounded again:

[You have become an official challenger.]

[All features available to challengers are unlocked.]

[The Horror Shop is unlocked...]

[The attribute panel is unlocked...]

[The friend function is unlocked...]

[The auction function is unlocked...]

[...]

[...]

[...]

A whole bunch of features are unlocked.

Lin Bei immediately opened the Horror Shop, searching through its dazzling array of goods.

Currently, he has over 35 million Horror Coins.

Enough to buy a decent item.

“I’ll buy something to boost my strength first.”

“What I lack right now is strength!”

The Horror Shop has so many items that it’s difficult to find something you like quickly.

Therefore, Lin Bei can only search slowly.

He sat on the sofa, patiently searching.

A minute later, Lin Bei found a suitable item.

“Elementary God-Demon Body?”

“This thing seems pretty good!”

He chose to view the details.

【Elementary God-Demon Body】

【Price: 35 million Horror Coins】

【Requirements: Healthy human】

【Product Description: After purchase, you will obtain a body comparable to a god or demon (Elementary), invulnerability to blades and spears (Elementary), immunity to all magic (Elementary), greatly increased strength and speed (Elementary), and the ability for your body’s cells and flesh to regenerate (Elementary). Even if a hand is severed and cannot be stitched back together, a new hand can grow given enough time.】

【Note: Further upgrades are possible…】

Lin Bei really liked this product.

Without hesitation, he selected to purchase.

【Purchase Successful.】

【This purchase amount is 35 million Terror Coins.】

【The Initial God-Demon Body is fusing...】

Lin Bei could clearly feel his body growing stronger at an extremely rapid pace.

His skin, muscles, bones, blood, cells, and organs were all strengthening.

Various impurities within his body were being expelled, and black filth fell to the ground.

The feeling was amazing; he closed his eyes and savored it.

After a while, the feeling dissipated, clearly indicating the fusion was complete.

Lin Bei opened his eyes, observing his body.

He was noticeably stronger.

Previously, his physique was quite ordinary; he was neither fat nor thin, with a little muscle.

But now, he had muscles all over his body—the kind of perfectly proportioned muscles that could unleash tremendous power without compromising his appearance; not the kind of monstrous, muscular physique.

On the surface, it was just that he had become stronger and gained more muscle.

But the actual changes were more than that.

Lin Bei used another function.

Attribute Panel...

He could view his current attributes.

Every data point accurately reflected his current situation.

【Lin Bei】

【Level: Wandering Soul】

[Strength: 19+100 (Divine Demon Body)]

[Constitution: 20+100]

[Spirit: 18+100]

[Speed: 19+100]

[Ability: Basic Divine Demon Body]

[Items: Berserk Bone-Shattering Saw, Random Vehicle Summoning Ticket, Repair Potion*5, Sanity Potion*5...]

The stats are relatively simple.

And it's not comprehensive enough.

"A lot of things aren't displayed on the panel."

"My two skills, 'Masterful Driver' and 'Mind Recovery,' and all the items I drew from the Lucky Roulette."

The system promptly provided an explanation.

[Because the Thriller World cannot detect the existence of this system, things the host obtains through the system—items, skills, attribute points, etc.—will not be displayed in the attribute panel.]

[This is very beneficial to the host!]

[Challengers can view other challengers' attribute panels through special means, but they cannot see the host's true panel.]

After listening, Lin Bei realized that this was indeed the case.

His attribute panel was highly deceptive.

But Lin Bei also noticed a problem.

"If it's like this, won't I also be unable to see my true attribute panel?"

"To be honest, it's a bit inconvenient!"

"System..."

"Think of a way!"

This was indeed a big problem.

Not being able to see his true attributes always made him feel awkward.

[Understood host's meaning.]

[Please wait patiently for a few minutes.]

[Forcibly invading the attribute panel function.]

[The host can do other things during this time.]

The system was more cooperative than Lin Bei had expected.

He was very satisfied.

"How many attribute points do I have now?"

[One hundred and two points!]

Lin Bei raised an eyebrow.

"So many?"

"Did I just increase the favorability of one hundred and two strange creatures?"

He hadn't counted at all.

As long as the previous favorability point reached one hundred, it would immediately become the next target.

He hadn't expected the final total to exceed one hundred.

"Not bad."

"Very efficient!"

"Keep up the good work after you get used to the situation!"

"Judging from what we just saw, there are quite a few malevolent-level anomalies in the safe zone, including the Rose Twins now."

"They're all targets to increase your favorability with!"

Lin Bei didn't consider the specifics for the time being.

He just wanted to use the attribute point allocation function.

"System, put all your attribute points into my Strength."

"A normal Soul-level character's attributes are around twenty, but because of my Divine Demon Body, all my attributes have increased by a hundred points."

"I'm already far ahead of challengers at the same level..."

[Ding!]

[Attribute Points Completed!]

With that, Lin Bei had finished everything he needed to do.

He sat on the sofa, waiting for the system to complete its intrusion and modifications.

During this time, Lin Bei took out the Superhuman Potion he had drawn on his first ten-draw from the Golden Roulette.

"After drinking it, the host's body will be greatly enhanced."

"Drink it first!"

Glug~

A bottle of Superhuman Potion went down his throat.

A warm current surged through Lin Bei's body.

His strength increased once again.

...

At the same time, in Gray Abyss City...

The one hundred and two monsters whose favorability Lin Bei had increased to one hundred were now in a frenzied state.

They were only stunned for a moment before Lin Bei vanished into thin air, without leaving a single command.

His disappearance was utterly inexplicable!

This worried and unsettled them, fearing Lin Bei had encountered some kind of danger.

"Where is he? Where did he go? Who's behind this?"

"Damn bastard, give him back to us, or we'll kill you even if it costs us our lives!"

"You unlucky thing, you wait, we'll definitely find you!"

"No matter who you are, you'll pay the price for daring to even touch him!"

"..."

The 102 bizarre creatures cursed wildly.

They just wanted Lin Bei back, they just wanted to be by his side.

Not far away, the Observer Volcano, who hadn't left yet, witnessed this frenzied scene.

It felt its scalp tingle...

It had offended over a hundred bizarre creatures at once.

Although their strength was all quite average.

"This is truly terrifying."

"I need to get out of here quickly."

"Fortunately, only these weaker monsters will be affected by him."

Thinking this, the Overseer, Volcano, sped away.

It still had many things to do.

It didn't want to clash with these crazy monsters.

...

A few minutes later, the system notification sounded:

【Ding!】

【This system has forcibly modified the host's attribute panel function, unilaterally fulfilling the requirement that the host can view all attributes, while maintaining concealment from the outside world. Other challengers, monsters, and the horror world cannot see the host's true attribute panel.】

【Each hidden piece of information will be marked with an asterisk (*).】

Lin Bei immediately checked his own attribute panel.

【Lin Bei】

【Level: Wanderer】

【Strength: 19 + 100 (Divine Demon Body) + 20 (Superhuman Potion) * 102 (Attribute Points)*】

【Constitution: 20 + 100 + 20*】

【Spirit: 18 + 100 + 20*】

【Speed: 19 + 100 + 20*】

【Abilities: Beginner Divine Demon Body, Masterful Driver*, Mind Recovery*...】

Lin Bei wore a smile.

“Although I’m only a Wanderer, the power I can actually unleash is far beyond that of a Wanderer.”

"Awesome!"



Chapter 34 Enjoying Life, The Unhappy White Rose


Lin Bei desperately wanted to test his strength against a monster.

But the situation didn't allow it.

This was a safe zone; he couldn't fight recklessly.

"I'll rest for a few days."

"We'll talk about other things during the next round of the job challenge."

"My first challenge rating was S-rank..."

"The next round's job challenge will definitely be more difficult; there's a high probability of encountering situations requiring combat."

Lin Bei knew he couldn't rely solely on the system.

After all, the system could only raise the favorability of one monster at a time.

What if he faced many monsters at once?

Dozens, hundreds...

And all of them wanted to attack him?

Wouldn't that be disastrous?

Even if he selected a monster to raise its favorability, it would still take at least a hundred seconds to reach one hundred.

Too many variables during that time!

Therefore, he pursued strength.

Lin Bei regained his composure, adjusted his state, and then began to observe his room.

Although it's a one-bedroom, one-living room, one-bathroom apartment, the actual area is quite large, at least 150 square meters, and fully furnished with all necessary appliances.

TV, refrigerator, washing machine, air conditioner, high-end computer, cooking machine, dishwasher, range hood...

It can be described as having everything.

You could easily spend ten days comfortably.

"Let's enjoy this first."

"Every challenge is a life-or-death struggle; I can't stay on edge all the time."

Lin Bei walked to the refrigerator, intending to check what food was inside.

The moment his hand touched the refrigerator, a message entered his mind—a detailed description of the room.

For ten Terror Coins, you can make the refrigerator stock various foods: eggs, premium beef, salmon, lobster, drinks, beer, expensive wines, candies and pastries, etc. Any food can appear, raw or cooked, entirely up to you. It's limited to normal foods, excluding special foods.

Other appliances are similar.

For example, the television—after paying one Terror Coin, you can watch any program, even restricted ones.

Air conditioning, computers, and electric cooking machines, among other appliances, also require payment in Terror Coins to use, with a flat price of one Terror Coin each—a relatively reasonable price.

Besides these, there are services like room cleaning and remote ordering.

Of course, these also require Terror Coins!

"To put it bluntly, as long as you have enough Terror Coins, you can enjoy a version of a rich man's life without freedom, eating and drinking as much as you want. It only costs a few dozen Terror Coins a day at most."

Lin Bei readily paid ten Terror Coins, mentally considering all sorts of foods he wanted to eat.

A second later, he opened the refrigerator door.

The refrigerator was full of food: meat, vegetables, eggs, dairy, various drinks, and alcohol.

Lin Bei took some out and placed it on the dining table, then devoured it.

After finishing, he didn't plan to clean up, since room cleaning was available.

Sitting on the sofa, Lin Bei let out a satisfied burp, watching his favorite program on the large-screen TV.

He was enjoying life.

...

Several hours passed in the blink of an eye,

Lin Bei finished his second meal.

Satiated and with the temperature just right, he couldn't help but feel a little restless.

As the saying goes...

"When you're well-fed and clothed, you think about..."

"What am I thinking?"

"It's all Yu Mei's fault!"

"But speaking of which, it's been a few days since I've seen Yu Mei. I wonder when I'll see her again."

"I actually miss her a little..."

Muttering to himself, Lin Bei looked around the room and found it had become dirty and messy, which was really affecting his mood.

"I'll order room cleaning service."

"It's only ten Terror Coins anyway."

"I wonder if it will be them!"

His desire to clean the room was just an excuse.

His real purpose was to try and see if he could see the Rose Twins again.

The initial encounter was too sudden, the information in the information panel was too explosive, and the Rose Twins had only taken the elevator up one floor. The time they spent together in the same space was too short, leaving Lin Bei no time to do anything.

The only saving grace was that the information panel revealed the Rose Twins' current identities.

Housekeeping!!

Compared to the other jobs in the safe zone being bizarre, contacting the two cleaners was relatively easy.

Lin Bei acted immediately.

He immediately placed an order for room cleaning service.

Ten Terror Coins deducted.

Then, he began to wait.

...

Meanwhile, the Rose Twins were in the lounge, having just finished cleaning a room and talking quietly.

"I wonder when my injuries will heal. Do I have to stay hidden here as a cleaner forever? I know it's safe this way, but it feels so stifling. We used to be so successful, sigh!"

"Sister, I know you're suffering, but no matter how much it hurts, you have to endure it. We can't be exposed until our injuries are fully healed, otherwise those guys will find us by the scent. In this state, if we encounter them, we'll both be dead."

"I understand, I'm just so unwilling!"

"Bear with it, it's better for both of us."

"..."

Suddenly, a mechanical voice sounded.

[Cleaner Xiaobai, you have a new room cleaning order.]

[Please arrive within ten minutes.]

[Room number: 1414.]

The sisters' conversation was interrupted.

Cleaning worker Xiaobai, actually White Rose, reluctantly stood up and walked towards the handcart piled with various cleaning tools.

Her delicate and beautiful face was filled with annoyance.

"Here we go again."

"Ten Terror Coins in total, not even enough to feed a beggar, yet I have to clean an apartment of over 100 square meters and collect mountains of garbage."

"If it were before, I wouldn't have hesitated to throw away even 100,000 Terror Coins, let alone a mere ten.

"Sigh, a tiger fallen into the plains is bullied by dogs!"

Despite her displeasure, cleaning worker Xiaobai's body language betrayed her, pushing the handcart towards the elevator.

Red Rose watched from behind.

She was miserable...

She disliked this situation as much.

Since infiltrating the safe zone, Red Rose hadn't had a single happy day.

Every day was unbearable, each day felt like an eternity!

“Sister, I’m sorry.”

“Don’t worry…”

“One day, I will take you away from here and back to our garden!!”

…

White Rose was very efficient.

She was on the elevator in less than half a minute.

But her reluctance was even more pronounced, as if she was about to do something she particularly hated.

“Ugh, this is so annoying!”

"I'm a Nightmare-level supernatural being! In any supernatural city, I'd be an absolute powerhouse, followed by at least eight or ten thousand supernatural beings. I used to live a life of luxury, but now I'm relegated to cleaning, tidying up rooms for a bunch of humans as weak as ants every day."

"Annoying!"

White Rose just complained.

She would still do what she had to do.

...

Ding-dong~

The doorbell rang.

Lin Bei sat up with a jolt, quickly went to the door, and looked outside through the peephole.

Standing outside was none other than the supernatural being he wanted to see!

White Rose, the younger sister of the Rose Twins!

"It really is her."

Taking a deep breath, Lin Bei opened the door.

He officially saw White Rose for the second time.

White Rose's information panel appeared.

[Cleaner - Xiaobai (White Rose from the Rose Twins)]

[Affection Points: -38]

[Relationship: You've made her very unhappy]

[Level: Vicious (Severely Injured, Originally Nightmare Level)]

[

She was very unhappy!

She came here to work as a cleaner only to recover from her injuries and seek refuge; she never intended to become a real cleaner.

But she had no choice.

To avoid attracting attention, she could only suppress her annoyance and frustration as she cleaned one filthy, messy house after another.

What's more...

You only paid her ten Terror Coins!

Not even enough for a meal.

So, she was very unhappy.

It's best not to mess with her!

】



Chapter 35 The White Rose's Resentment, the Astonished Red Rose


Lin Bei was speechless.

Was it really that big of a deal?

It was just cleaning a room, a matter of ten or twenty minutes.

It's such a small thing, is it really necessary to be this upset?

He'd lost thirty-eight points of goodwill!

And he was still so unhappy…

He was internally complaining, but outwardly remained calm, maintaining a normal demeanor, and stepped aside with a faint smile.

Bai Meigui still looked displeased.

She pushed a cart full of tools into room 1414.

As soon as she entered the room, she immediately began to survey it, analyzing which areas needed cleaning.

The next moment, she almost fainted.

"My God!"

Because it was so dirty!

It was visually striking!

The table and surrounding area were littered with food scraps and packaging. Even though Lin Bei hadn't intentionally wasted food, he had eaten an unusually large amount, inevitably resulting in crumbs, spilled soup, and oil stains.

The reason for this chaotic scene was that Lin Bei's appetite had suddenly become enormous.

At least ten times that of an ordinary person.

Lin Bei also figured out the reason.

Because of the Divine Demon Body!!

After fusing with the Divine Demon Body, his physical attributes had indeed improved significantly, but there was also a side effect.

His appetite had exploded!

No…

To be precise, his body's need for various nutrients and substances had increased dramatically. A normal person could be full from one bowl of rice, but he would need to eat at least ten bowls.

If a normal person could get enough nutrients from one vitamin, he would need to take ten at once…

After all, it was only a rudimentary Divine Demon Body; it was impossible for it to be without side effects.

"So dirty, so messy..."

"It's all leftover food!"

"So disgusting!"

"Help, I'm going crazy!"

White Rose mentally screamed.

She continued to observe.

Soon, another filthy and messy area was discovered.

The sofa area...

The floor was covered in foul-smelling black specks and a black, sticky liquid of unknown origin, even more disgusting than the leftover food, and clearly difficult to clean.

Other areas were relatively clean, but still needed some tidying up.

“Bastard!”

“You’ve only been here a few hours and you’ve turned the room into this mess?”

“If this were before, I would have made you fertilizer for the plants!”

Lin Bei kept staring at White Rose.

White Rose’s information panel changed.

Her favorability decreased again...

【Cleaner - Xiao Bai (White Rose of the Rose Twins)】

【Favorability Points: -50】

The fact that I was supposed to clean your room already made her very unhappy.

Filthy!

Messy!

Disgusting!

But she knows she can't.

So she's enduring it.

She can only endure it!!

However, she'll remember you.

If she ever sees you outside the safe zone, she'll show you what cruelty truly is!

Looking at this message, Lin Bei felt a chill run down his spine.

Good heavens...

She's being held against her!!

A nightmare-level eeriness!!

“No.”

“You forced me to do this.”

Lin Bei’s mind was made up.

At this moment, a deeply disgruntled White Rose walked to the dining table with a gloomy face, reluctantly cleaning up the mess Lin Bei had left behind.

As she cleaned, she cursed inwardly.

“Disgusting, so disgusting! All the humans living here are so annoying!”

“Eating this much over several hours, are you a pig?”

“You eat and drink to your heart’s content, lying there comfortably, while I have to clean up this mess, only to receive a paltry ten Terror Coins. Doesn’t your conscience hurt?”

“Damn it, you really deserve to die!”

Her goodwill towards her continued to decrease.

[Cleaner - Xiaobai]

[Affection Points: -55]

[...]

She's scolding you...

】

【..】

[Affection Points: -66]

Her profanity was too much to handle!

Although she didn't verbally curse, White Rose wasn't just cursing in her heart; she was also protesting with her actions.

She deliberately made a lot of noise.

Clanging and banging.

Or crushing bottles.

Or vigorously scrubbing and slamming the table.

Or deliberately dropping some trash on the floor and stomping on it.

Using the mop and broom like butcher knives, they moved with ferocious force, their murderous intent palpable.

If the furniture hadn't been of good quality, who knows how badly it would have been destroyed.
