She and White Rose were twin sisters, possessing an innate and extremely strong connection, able to clearly sense each other's emotional states.

No one understands the white rose better than the red rose.

【Cleaner - Xiaobai】

【Favorability Points: -80】

【...】

【

Her mood is getting worse...

She feels like you're a piece of trash!

If this continues, won't the favorability drop to negative 100?

【Ding!】

+1…

It's just a few minutes, what's the rush?

+1...

Affection level dropped to zero.

[Cleaner - Novice]

[Relationship: Calm and Peaceful]

[Level: Vicious (Severely Injured, Originally Nightmare Level)]

【

She suddenly stopped being angry.

It was as if she had accepted the reality that she was now a cleaner.

Even though she was surrounded by the garbage you had created, she didn't complain or seem upset.

She just wanted to finish tidying up the house quickly and leave.

She doesn't dislike you, but she doesn't want to spend too much time with you either; you're just a guest to her.

Even numb.

Completely different from before.

What could possibly make a particularly angry monster calm down in such a short time?

Is this level of bad mood so easy to handle?

At least she had never experienced it!



Chapter 36 Little Sister, what's wrong with you?


“I’ll have to ask my sister when she gets back.”

“I’m very interested.”

“How can one instantly change their mood from gloomy to sunny?”

Red Rose didn’t know.

This was just the beginning…

The real show was about to begin!

…

Lin Bei sat on the sofa, holding a cup of goji berry and ginseng eight-treasure tea, sipping it slowly.

The ginseng and similar substances in the horror world were very pure; drinking it made him feel warm.

His attention remained focused on White Rose, who was tidying up in the dining area.

A “+1” number kept appearing and disappearing above White Rose’s head.

+1…

+1…

The frequency was once per second.

Ten seconds passed, and White Rose’s favorability had increased to ten.

Chapter 46 The Suit-Wearing Thug Goes Berserk, The Long-Awaited Opportunity

Bang!

Thump!

Another kick.

The monster's body contorted from the kick, and it vomited up the food it had eaten the day before yesterday.

"Still so weak."

The Bloodthirsty Bunny Girl continued.

The long, slender, snow-white legs moved so fast they left afterimages.

Longer than the lifespan of an old man!

Whiter than the snow under the Arctic Circle!

Being kicked by them hurts more than being hit by a train!

She was arrogant.

Simply because they hadn’t received Lin Bei’s permission.

The Bloodstained Bunny Girl was also furious.

Her good mood was ruined.

【Bloodthirsty Bunny Girl - Squeak】

【Affection Points: -10】

Chapter 61 I like you, I want you both physically and mentally.

Chapter 62 Zhizhi, who loves fishing, you call me Second Sister-in-law?

Chapter 63 It's my fault for meeting you too late; I'm here for fair competition.

Meanwhile, at Mingfeng Residence.

Chapter 64 The Loli's Split and True Injury, The Struggle Between Sisters-in-Law

SS-level wanted monster!!

Chapter 65 Squeaky and Rain Fairy, the Hundred-Mouthed Glutton

Chapter 68 You're a professional at lying to your father, Actress Ten Nights of Fragrance

'Please find her as soon as possible, preferably captured alive.'

'Anyone, whether an employee or not, is welcome to participate.'

It was as if a thunderbolt exploded in her mind. The little girl froze, her large eyes filled with even deeper confusion. A tuft of hair stood on end, her twin ponytails slightly forked, as if struck by lightning.

Her understanding and worldview were violently shaken.

"Love...lover?"

"Little Mei, you...you've fallen in love with a human?"

"Are you sure you're not joking?"

The little girl couldn't believe it.

Love between a human and a demon has always been taboo.

It has happened many times over the years, but almost always ends badly.

Humans and demons are two different races, inherently incompatible in terms of values ​​and other aspects. The sweeter their relationship is in the early stages, the more severe the backlash will be later.

“It’s true.”

“I’m not kidding!”

“I love him!”

Yu Mei’s tone was absolutely certain, with not a trace of lying.

Seeing this, the little girl completely believed Yu Mei.

She didn’t understand this, and found it hard to agree with, but she could accept it, and she would bless Yu Mei.

“Then congratulations.”

“You’ve found your true love.”

“I hope you two will be happy and sweet forever.”

The little girl’s blessings were genuine.

She truly considered Yu Mei a friend.

Blessings aside, the little girl didn’t have much confidence.

“Thank you.”

Yu Mei replied with a smile.

She knew what the little girl was thinking, but she wasn’t worried about it at all, because she believed in her love for Lin Bei.

"The walls are all repaired?"

Yu Mei changed the subject.

"Yes."

"They're all back to how they were before."

"But why did all the monsters you kept locked up suddenly become unconscious?"

"I only closed my eyes for a few seconds, and when I opened them again, they were like this."

The little girl still didn't understand.

In her opinion, this had absolutely nothing to do with her; after all, she was just an ordinary repairman.

Yu Mei sighed helplessly.

“Never mind them.”

“Let them lie down if they want.”

“That’s not important.”

“I came back to find you because I have other things to do.”

Then, Yu Mei began to communicate with the little girl.

Lin Bei observed from the side.

The longer he observed, the more certain he became of one thing.

Besides in repair and construction, the little girl was clearly a rather clumsy ghost; she almost twisted her ankle after taking just a few steps…

At the same time, Lin Bei also confirmed something else.

The little girl genuinely didn’t know how strong she was…

Soon, Yu Mei and the little girl finished their communication.

The little girl readily agreed.

Because she had long wanted to visit the Underworld Office, mainly to see the structure of the various buildings, and if possible, she also wanted to make some appropriate modifications.

Regarding safety, the little girl wasn't unconcerned, but she hadn't thought about it yet, currently immersed in the excitement of visiting the office.

Afterwards, Lin Bei and the two ghosts waited there temporarily, also sending the ghosts back to their cells.

During this time, the little girl's affection remained stable, consistently at a value of 6.

...

Half an hour later, Lin Bei's mobile device rang; it was Zizhi contacting him.

Lin Bei answered the mobile device, and Zizhi's voice came from the other end.

"Husband~"

A sweet, coquettish voice rang out, audible to Lin Bei, Yu Mei, and the little girl.

At that moment, the little girl's pupils dilated in shock!

She hurriedly looked at Yu Mei, stammering as she spoke.

"You...you listen..."

"That female ghost over there is calling...calling him 'husband'!"

"Little Mei, you've been...you've been cheated on!!"

"He's a scumbag!"

But to the little girl's surprise, Yu Mei remained calm, completely unaffected by the situation.

She simply nodded.

"Calm down."

"I already knew."

"That guy over there is competing with me for the position of sister-in-law. She's a formidable competitor."

"But you don't need to worry. The position of sister-in-law is definitely mine."

"I came to you for help to solidify my position."

After hearing this, the little girl felt her worldview, built up over many years, completely collapse!

She was almost going crazy!!

Being cheated on by a mistress and remaining calm was one thing.

But competing with a mistress for the position of sister-in-law—what kind of outrageous behavior is that?

When did the world become like this?

A mistress dares to challenge the wife?!

Just then, Yu Mei continued:

"And I can confirm it."

"She's not the only one vying with me for the position of sister-in-law; there are probably many other female ghosts."

"She's just the first one I've encountered."

The little girl's head began to throb.

The amount of information was overwhelming; her brain couldn't process it all.

"No..."

"I need to go outside and calm down."

"Your world is so hard to understand..."

With that, the little girl, feeling dizzy, went outside the dungeon.

Yu Mei kept watching the little girl.

"I hope you never understand."

"Don't get involved in this bloody struggle."

She was worried the little girl would join in too.

If she did, they really wouldn't be able to win!

...



Chapter 73 Explosive Popping Candy, Even the Little Girl Wants Some


Lin Bei was communicating with Zhizhi.

Zhizhi would arrive in about ten minutes, bringing with her an S-rank wanted monster.

"Ten minutes later, go find Yu Mei."

Yu Mei observed her surroundings.

The captured monsters were all unconscious. The little girl was too shocked to leave and went outside to clear her head, leaving only her and Lin Bei in the dungeon.

Recalling her argument with Zhizhi a few hours earlier, she wanted to expand her existing advantage and further widen the gap between them. So she turned to Lin Bei, her delicate little hand taking his large one.

"Brother~"

Although it was just a single call, Yu Mei's meaning was perfectly conveyed.

Lin Bei was startled.

"Wait..."

"Only ten minutes."

"That's not enough."

He wasn't afraid or tired; he simply didn't have enough time.

Yu Mei had also thought of this.

A bottle of ice water, a bag of popping candy, and a pack of tissues.

All were specialties from the horror world.

The ice water packaging clearly stated that the water source was the River Styx.

The popping candy had several red words written on it: "Absolutely explosive, popping wildly, let a heavy metal band hold a concert in your mouth."

The tissue packaging was even more outrageous: "This tissue is professionally designed for destroying evidence, perfectly erasing your crimes."

Lin Bei was dumbfounded...

"???"

He never expected Yu Mei to buy these things.

She used that short waiting time to make a special trip to the store?

And these three items were all a bit strange!

Ice water from the River Styx?

Popping candy for a concert?

Tissues for covering up a corpse?

Huh?

Are you kidding me?

Now that things have come to this, how could Lin Bei refuse?

"Okay..."

"Then as you wish."

Upon receiving a positive answer, Yu Mei cheered inwardly:

"Yay~"

At this moment, Lin Bei asked a question.

His intuition told him that Zhizhi wouldn't let Yu Mei move freely unless she also had something to do."

Yu Mei answered while preparing herself.

The little girl strolled outside, wandering among the graves.

After distancing herself from Yu Mei and Lin Bei, her already not-so-bright mind gradually returned to normal.

No matter how she thought about it, she couldn't understand...

To avoid ending up like that, the little girl kept reminding herself.

Squeaky soon arrived at the mass grave. She moved slowly, her right hand still gripping a leg, dragging a strange creature along.

A little girl, who happened to be taking a walk nearby, saw Zizi dragging a strange figure towards her.

"Indeed a strong competitor."

At the same time, Zizi also saw the little girl.

A particularly cute-looking female ghost wandering around such a remote mass grave in the middle of the night seemed absurd.

The first impression a little girl gives to ghosts is that she's soft, cute, and easy to bully.

Zizi didn't speak to the little girl.

They continued walking towards the dungeon.

The little girl lost interest in continuing her stroll and followed Zizi towards the dungeon from a distance.

The severely injured and unconscious monster was dragged along by Zizi.

This directly resulted in Zizi having a poor image in the little girl's mind.

"How scary..."

"She'll definitely be hard to get along with."

Just then, as Zizi and Yumei reached the dungeon, a refreshed Lin Bei and a shyly satisfied Yumei emerged from the dungeon.

The corresponding information panel appeared before him:

[Iron-Headed Monster]

[Affection Points: 0]

[Relationship: Neutral]

[Rank: Evil Monster]

[...]

He is an S-rank wanted criminal.

He's quite capable, enjoys doing bad things, and is always causing trouble, always managing to escape danger by sheer luck.

But today, things have changed.

Zizi took the initiative to find him, beat him up, knocked him unconscious, and then forcibly brought him here.

Chapter 74: You stuffed him in the trunk, and you caught four at once?

Both older sisters were fierce.

Having the little girl in the passenger seat and the other monsters in the back would solve the problem.

Lin Bei had spoken, and Yu Mei and Zhi Zhi stopped arguing, their gazes simultaneously falling on Iron-Headed Monster.

A rope, tied to four bizarre creatures.

Rain Demon, Loli, and Squeak.

Extremely eye-catching!

After saying that, she noticed the long list of wanted monsters behind Lin Bei.

The bounty hunter was stunned.

She knew Lin Bei's plan, but she didn't expect him to go to such lengths...

"My God!"

"Plus the reward system I just created for him, he can get 15 million Terror Coins in one go..."

"Just six or seven times, and you can get a hundred million..."

The bounty hunter was genuinely shocked.

She knew very well that

doing this repeatedly would definitely cause a huge change.

However, it would take some time.

"How so?"

"If not, I'll go catch a few more."

The bounty hunter quickly replied.

"Enough...enough."

"I wouldn't dare do any more."

She suddenly felt sorry for the Underworld Office in Gray Abyss City.

They'd encountered Lin Bei, this temporary worker.

He could catch many wanted monsters, but all he cared about was fleecing them...

"I'll transfer the bounty to you right away."

"Their total bounty is twelve million. Adding the reward plan I just devised specifically for you, the final bounty will be fifteen million."

"Are you satisfied with this amount?"

"If not, I'll add some more..."



Chapter 75 The little girl is terrified; how was she captured?


"Fifteen million?"

After all that work, he only got fifteen million?

Since it was Lin Bei, she was more than willing.

The necessary procedures still had to be followed; it would be easier to cover things up later if anyone questioned it.

Zhizhi and Yumei couldn't help but exchange a glance, seeing the same meaning in each other's eyes.

The Rain Night Butcher, a ruthless killer, specifically targeted wealthy monsters; being targeted by her meant certain death, a agonizing end.

Let's not talk about it...

Chapter 76 is truly a model for us all; the best way to make amends.

Lin Bei, still holding the chainsaw, also left the Underworld Office, leaving behind only a group of onlookers stunned by Lin Bei's courage.

"That guy's really something! Carrying a chainsaw and daring to chase those four monsters? Doesn't he know how terrifying they are?"

"He's a true role model!"

Lin Bei arrived.

Looking at Yu Mei and Zhizhi, he said:

"Not bad."

"The plan went smoothly."

"Rest now, wait for news of the bounty."

The news of multiple monsters being captured and then escaping spread rapidly.

The higher-ups at the Gray Abyss City Underworld Office naturally learned of it.

Almost immediately, all the relevant monsters were summoned to a meeting.

In the meeting room, the true manager of the Gray Abyss City Underworld Office looked grim.

Since the bounty was issued, nothing like this had ever happened...

She couldn't even imagine the magnitude of the impact this would have.

It was tantamount to a devastating blow to the head.

"Regarding this matter..."

"Does anyone have anything to say?"

The words fell, but there was no response, an eerie silence.

The manager waited.

One minute...

Two minutes...

Still no movement.

She could only speak again:

"I'm not here to settle scores."

"Speak whatever you have to say."

But still no response.

Seeing this, the manager had no choice but to call on someone:

"How do you think this should be handled?"

Bounty Hunter immediately stood up, her face showing no panic.

Actually, she really wanted to speak first, but to avoid arousing suspicion, she could only suppress this urge.

Now that the opportunity had come, Bounty Hunter decisively spoke.

“I think we should increase the bounty on those four strange creatures and appropriately raise their bounty levels.”

“As you probably know, the news of multiple strange creatures successfully escaping will have a serious impact. Many members might lose trust in the office, and some might even quit. So we must do everything we can to remedy the situation!”

“Increase the bounty on those four, get more strange creatures and humans to capture them, preferably as quickly as possible. This is the only way to effectively recover our losses.”

“And to do this, the reward must be substantial.”

“Also, we need to get rid of that lousy restaurant. This problem arose because we ate there.”

“These are my suggestions.”

She had just finished speaking.

Xiaotian, who had also been called to the meeting, stated:

“I support this suggestion.”

“Obviously, this is the best method.”

Then, the four strange creatures, including Kuangxiao, who had barely recovered after taking their medicine, also voiced their opinions.

"I agree, we need to bring them back as soon as possible!"

"It'd be best to double the bounty, otherwise it won't be very effective!"

"Yes, that restaurant needs to be taken down too."

"..."

Seeing that more than half of the monsters agreed with Bounty Hunter's suggestion, the manager began to consider the feasibility of this method.

After thinking for more than ten seconds, she realized that this was indeed the only effective and best method.

The four escaped monsters, led by Rain Demon, must be brought back!

"Okay."

"Let's do it this way."

"Increase their bounty level and reward."

"Bounty Hunter, you are fully responsible for how to execute this!"

"I only have one requirement..."

"The impact of this incident must be minimized!"

"Understood?"

Bounty Hunter was very excited.

The opportunity for Lin Bei to make a fortune had arrived.

"Okay, leave it to me."

"I guarantee I can bring them back in the shortest time possible."

Just as she finished speaking, the four eerie creatures, laughing maniacally, added:

"Please let us continue to handle the escort. This time we'll eat at a different restaurant; there won't be any more stomach aches."

"Please trust us; there won't be any mistakes next time."

"It's our fault! We want to atone for our sins!"

"..."

Xiaotian already knew what was going on.

Seeing this, he seized the opportunity to persuade them:

"I also think we should give them another chance!"

“Since those four monsters escaped because they had stomach problems, then having them take them to the detention room is the most correct course of action!”

“And I will join them and participate in the handover!”

“…”

The other monsters were successfully swayed.

They began to support the suggestion from Bounty Hunter and the other monsters.

“That sounds reasonable; it should indeed be done that way.”

“I agree.”

“…”

Finally, the administrator of the Underworld Office in Gray Abyss City made up her mind.

“Okay.”

“We’ll do it your way.”

After thinking it over, she felt this method was very suitable and couldn’t see any problems with it.

Chapter 80 The Underworld Office Goes Bankrupt? Regulators Come Knocking


The movie's runtime passed quickly.

Lin Bei stood by the window, a cigarette in his hand, taking a deep drag with a look of pure enjoyment.

Better than a god!

Yin Yueying, having changed from her pajamas into a short skirt, sat obediently on the sofa, watching Lin Bei's back with eyes full of adoration.

“I’ll transfer all my Terror Coins to you after I’ve rested.”

“You can continue what you’re doing. Don’t worry about trouble coming your way. I can handle everything. Just go ahead and do it.”

“And after that, with Terror Coins, I’ll transfer everything except for necessary expenses to you!”

Actually, Yin Yueying didn’t want Lin Bei to do things that carried a certain degree of risk.

She wanted to pamper Lin Bei, providing him with the best food and drink.

Ideally, he could be by her side every day.

She would give him whatever he wanted!

But Yin Yueying also knew very well that this was impossible.

So she had no choice but to compromise with Lin Bei.

She would support Lin Bei while also covering for him.

As for how much money the Underworld Office in Gray Abyss City would waste, Yin Yueying didn't care at all.

Lin Bei didn't refuse.

He would accept the Terror Coins the female ghosts offered, and he wouldn't miss out on Terror Coins he could obtain through other means.

The reason was simple.

He was desperately short!

Even several hundred million Terror Coins wouldn't last long.

Buying a few decent items would cost them all.

Some things he couldn't even afford!

That's why Lin Bei did these things.

He didn't want to rely on the female ghosts for everything.

Um...

It wasn't that he felt it was inappropriate, nor that he felt he was losing face, but rather to prevent unforeseen circumstances.

In case of a dangerous situation one day, without any high-favorability monsters around, and unable to improve the favorability of the opposing monster, then one can only rely on one's own strength.

Therefore, personal strength is also very important.

It's optional, but it's essential.

"I only have a little over three hundred million Terror Coins right now."

"Far from enough."

"Just buying the complete Thor's Bloodline would cost two hundred million..."

The Thor's Bloodline...

It was a product Lin Bei had long coveted.

After fusion, one could obtain the bloodline of the Thor, mastering various forms of lightning power, from the most ordinary lightning to death lightning that could strip away life, and even divine lightning capable of destroying everything.

There were many ways to use it; it could be released for attack, or the lightning could flow in one's blood to increase speed and power, etc...

Chapter 84 After spending the night at Mingfeng's place, the little girl decides to join.


The little girl had a bad feeling.

Even if Lin Bei did nothing, and barely spoke to her, the little girl wouldn't let her guard down.

It was too sudden; without any warning, he threw something out, as if it were a spur-of-the-moment act...

Lin Bei, on the other hand, continued to sit there, showing no sign of anything amiss, speaking as calmly as ever.

"Didn't you see?"

"I threw a talisman into your body."

"Don't worry, it's not a bad thing, it just means your strength will temporarily decrease by one level."

【Master Craftswoman - Little Girl】

100,000 Terror Coins?

"As a full-fledged employee of the Underworld Office, I can freely enter and exit every Underworld Office."

"The Sin Buster Medal and the Star of Honor title are both bonus items. The medal increases my bounty earnings, and the title increases my points."

"The wanted list is nothing special."

"The rest are the Mind Evolution Gun and the Decapitation Axe."

"Two decent Underworld weapons, a pistol and an axe."

"The Mind Evolution Gun, a B-grade Underworld weapon, can produce Holy Water bullets, with a single storage limit of fifty. Holy Water bullets deal high damage to the supernatural, making it a powerful weapon for killing them."

"The Decapitation Axe, a B-grade Underworld weapon, is specifically designed for decapitation. It's exceptionally sharp, cutting through iron like mud, and has a bonus when striking the head."

Overall, not bad.

"Although they're only two B-grade Netherworld artifacts."

"They're still usable."

"Let's keep them for now."

...

Lin Bei checked his current Terror Coin balance.

1.3128 billion.

"Let's get the Thor's Bloodline first."

The Thor's Body...

He'd been coveting it for a long time.

[Thor's Bloodline Full Version]

[Price: 200 million Terror Coins]

[Usage Requirements: Must be alive]

[Product Description: After purchase, you can fuse with the bloodline of the ancient Thunder God, gaining the power to control countless bolts of lightning. This power can be used for attack or support, and can even imbue every cell with lightning, making it an exceptionally powerful bloodline.]

[Note: This is the most complete bloodline; further enhancement is impossible, but it can be fused with other bloodlines...]

The description was simple.

But Lin Bei's decision remained unchanged.

Buy directly!

[Purchase successful.]

[This purchase costs 200 million Terror Coins.]

[The Thunder God bloodline is fusing.]

Boom!

A deafening thunderclap resounded, and seven-colored lightning burst forth from Lin Bei's body and exterior, wantonly destroying everything around him. Multiple charred holes appeared in the walls, the floor, and the furniture.

He closed his eyes.

Sensing all the changes.

The blood flowing through his body had become entirely lightning; his heart, kidneys, and other organs were enveloped in layers of lightning, and every cell received the power of lightning.

Even every single hair on Lin Bei's head crackled with lightning.

He tried to control the lightning within his body.

It was as easy as controlling his limbs; he could direct the lightning wherever he wanted it to gather.

Lin Bei first tried gathering lightning in his left hand.

Success.

His right hand, success.

His legs, success.

The Mysterious Land, success!

Um...

The best auxiliary method for increasing affection.

[Advanced Divine Demon Body]

Lin Bei's physique has strengthened once again.

However, his appearance hasn't changed much.

Lin Bei is preparing to buy another suitable Netherworld Artifact and is frantically searching the shop.

But it's quite difficult.

There are many powerful Netherworld Artifacts, but few meet his requirements.

Just then, the tightly closed door was suddenly pushed open.

Immediately afterward, a joyful cheer rang out.

"You're back~"

"So happy."

Lin Bei's thoughts were interrupted, and he subconsciously looked towards the doorway.

The one who had just walked in was none other than her younger sister, White Rose.

White Rose tossed aside the cart full of cleaning tools and rushed straight towards Lin Bei, carelessly tossing her cleaning coat on the ground.

When she reached Lin Bei, she was only wearing her undergarments.

A warm touch came.

The scent of roses filled the air.

White Rose's longing words rang out.

"I missed you so much~"

"I haven't seen you for days, I'm almost withered..."

"While you're not here, could you water me?"

Her physique had just undergone a significant boost and was at its peak.

And the Phoenix Dance's special meal was beginning to take effect...

Overwhelmed by a confluence of factors, Lin Bei felt an irrepressible urge to act.

He hoisted the white rose onto his shoulder and walked towards the room.

Passing the area opposite the door, Lin Bei casually released a palm-shaped bolt of lightning, gently closing and locking the door.

Then, he controlled the lightning, directing it towards a specific point.

Sizzle~

As he moved forward, sparks and lightning streaked across his path...

Unleashing an unparalleled power.

Boom!

Boom!!

Soon, thunder roared!

Lightning crackled throughout the room...

...

Lin Bei returned to the living room.

Chapter 89 A Lament for Thunder and Fire, A Rose Struck by Lightning


Looking at the long list of attributes, Lin Bei couldn't be sure what level his true strength had reached.

Strength exceeded 1000.

The other attributes were also approaching 1000.

While the base attributes of a Wraith-level being were all 50.

[Challengers gain experience by completing class challenges.]

【Elegy of Thunder and Fire】

【Price: 500 Million Horror Coins】

Different playing techniques can produce different effects; the power varies depending on the number of strings plucked each time.

Single-target precise attack, indiscriminate area attack, attacks that can be heard but not seen...

it creates a sonic field.

[Purchase successful.]

[This purchase amount is 500 million Terror Coins.]

The next moment, an electric guitar appeared in Lin Bei's hand.

It was predominantly purple.

Adorned with red and blue accents.

It resembled a neon-lit cyberpunk city at night, possessing a unique charm.

It was really cool!

Especially those six strings.

Completely different from normal electric guitar strings.

They were like chords, thin with lightning and flames.

The flames surged, the lightning danced.

The strings were installed one by one, intricately interwoven.

Lin Bei held the guitar in his left hand and gently plucked a string with his right.

A sound rang out.

The originally orange-yellow strings instantly turned purple.

Boom!

A wave of fiery sound appeared, spreading outwards.

The room began to melt, sparks appearing on the sofa and coffee table.

Seeing this, Lin Bei quickly stopped.

He was a little hesitant to play this electric guitar...

Otherwise, the consequences could be serious...

Anyway, Lin Bei was very satisfied with this SS-grade artifact.

Just then, a system notification suddenly sounded:

[The upgrade materials for this artifact are available in the Legendary-grade roulette wheel, and there is a certain probability of drawing it from the Diamond-grade roulette wheel.]

[The host can try if interested.]

Lin Bei noted this down.

As for trying?

The conditions didn't allow it; his favorability points were far from sufficient. He would have to accumulate them over time.

Fortunately, it was only a matter of time.

Given enough time, he could easily accumulate enough points.

...

At this point, he only had a little over two hundred million Terror Coins left.

Lin Bei didn't plan to spend them for the time being, as he hadn't found any suitable items.

He continued searching in the Terror Shop.

A few minutes later, the door to the room opened again.

Red Rose pushed in a tool cart.

As soon as she entered, she saw Lin Bei and did the same thing as White Rose.

She rushed towards Lin Bei.

After cuddling for a while, Red Rose remembered something.

"Where's my sister?"

"Is her car here?"

"But I haven't seen her."

She had been too excited and had forgotten about White Rose.

Lin Bei answered truthfully:

"She's sleeping inside."

For some reason, Red Rose could always smell a strange odor.

A faint smell of burning.

"Why is there this smell?"

"It's coming from the room..."

"I'll go check."

Although reluctant to leave Lin Bei's arms, for his safety, she forced herself to check.

What she saw completely stunned her.

White Rose lay there quietly asleep.

Occasionally, lightning would flicker across her body, and her skin seemed slightly darker.

The smell was emanating from White Rose.

Red Rose froze on the spot.

Her brain overloaded, almost crashing.

"Sister..."

"What happened?"

"Why does she smell burnt?"

"Why is there lightning?"

Lin Bei also walked over at this moment.

Red Rose's reaction was as he expected.

"How should I explain this..."

"Have you heard of lightning-struck wood?"

Red Rose nodded.

"Yes."

She knew about lightning-struck wood.

After all, it was a method for dealing with strange things.

"That explains it."

"You can think of her as a lightning-struck rose, only struck from within. But don't worry, this lightning strike is only beneficial."

Red Rose was still somewhat confused.

Her expression was puzzled, her eyes full of bewilderment.

"But..."

"This is a safe zone. Although it rains and snows sometimes, as long as you're indoors, you won't be affected. It's impossible for my sister to have been struck by lightning."

Lin Bei was silent for a moment.

Then he sighed.

Sigh~

"Is there a possibility..."

"She was struck by my lightning?"

*Zzzzt!*

A bolt of lightning danced in Lin Bei's palm, radiating dazzling light.

At that moment, Red Rose suddenly understood Lin Bei's meaning.

Her gaze shifted downwards.

*Zzzzt!*

Lightning surged.

Red Rose became excited, her face flushing slightly.

"Um..."

As long as her sister was alright.

An hour later...

Another lightning-struck rose appeared.

The two sisters were perfectly aligned...

It had started raining.

"I've done most of what I needed to do."

"Let's go to the hall."

"There's still something to do there."



Chapter 90: The Administrator is Stunned, Outside the Safe Zone


He took the elevator to the lobby.

Lin Bei casually found a seat and looked at the eerie creatures moving around in the lobby.

“System.”

“Initiating favorability increase.”

“Target: All the eerie creatures you can see.”

On the surface, he is the Warm Apartment administrator's capable assistant. Except for some particularly important matters requiring his approval, he basically makes all the decisions in the safe zone.

No matter what you ask for, he will do it at all costs.

Lin Bei knew that what existed outside the safe zone was the Black Mist, a bizarre core force at the level of a Ghost God. Entering the Black Mist without permission would result in being devoured.

Actually, he really wanted to go out and see.

The safe zone was very safe. As long as he stayed inside obediently and possessed a certain amount of Terror Coins, he could enjoy a life free from worries about food and drink.

However, Lin Bei felt something was strange.

This way of life was like being a pet kept in captivity, allowed to roam freely every ten days, and confined to a safe zone the rest of the time. Even going outside required permission from the administrator.

“Of course.”

“I’ll just talk to the administrator.”

Lin Bei nodded, indicating he understood.

Then, the chief assistant went to speak with the administrator.

“Are you crazy?”

“And it has to be him!”

The administrator frowned.

The chief assistant had never made such a request before.

He felt something was wrong.

But he couldn't quite put his finger on it.

“Yes.”

“I think it’s a very reasonable request.”

“It’s just going outside to take a look, it’s not an unreasonable thing.”

“You can’t possibly disagree with that, can you?”

“No matter what you think, I’m letting him go out for a walk today.”

The chief assistant’s attitude was firm.

Even if it meant losing his job, he had to satisfy Lin Bei.

Just then, the other strange beings in the hall gathered around, all speaking up for Lin Bei.

"The assistant is right. This request isn't unreasonable at all. It's just going out to take a look. You won't even agree to such a small request?"

He was incredibly frustrated!

Those who used to obey him obediently were now rebelling against him for the sake of a human, even threatening him.

Among them was his most trusted chief assistant!

“They’ve gone mad!”

He knew that if he didn’t let Lin Bei go outside to check things out today, the chief assistant and the other eerie entities would definitely not stop.

He had no other option.

Letting Lin Bei go outside for a walk wasn't a big deal; it was within the permitted limits.

This was even more outrageous than his dream of becoming a Buddha!

“He definitely has a serious problem!”

“But it’s also a matter of curiosity, after all, he’s in the safe zone I manage.”

“On the other hand, he’s my commodity. I can sell him to other safe zones at will, or to those big shots and weirdos!”

The Warm Apartment manager didn’t dislike Lin Bei.

In his eyes, Lin Bei had great value.

...

Lin Bei was enveloped in the black mist.

Now, truly outside the safe zone, he truly understood the mystery of this black mist.

The black mist enveloped his body.

The areas of skin in direct contact with the black mist felt a faint burning sensation, like tentacles slowly moving across his skin. A bone-chilling cold seeped into his bones, and strange whispers echoed in his ears. Lin Bei’s mind was reeling.

His breathing was labored, his limbs felt heavy, and taking a single step forward required immense effort.



Chapter 91 The Garden Master, The Third Challenge


"This really gave me a rude awakening."

Huff~

The situation at higher elevations was the same; he could only see windows with lights on and the occasional figure passing by.

But the lower areas were different, with some visibility.

Lin Bei was currently in a courtyard, planted with various unfamiliar flowers, plants, and trees, no different from an ordinary courtyard.

Around him were countless tall buildings, stretching as far as the eye could see.

Lin Bei endured the discomfort, and under the protection of the strange creatures, he wandered around the courtyard.

At the same time, he observed the strange flowers, plants, and trees.

Their appearances were all bizarre, and their colors were off.

It would only be more terrifying!

Splash!

Nothing changed.

A second later, the pure white dissipated, and Lin Bei disappeared.

The Chief Assistant and the other ghosts were the same.

The boat continued moving, and the Lake Woman sat quietly.

She looked towards the Warm Apartment complex.

“You…”

“You’re a little early.”

…

After his vision returned, the first thing Lin Bei saw was the familiar lobby, then the Warm Apartment manager, and then the strange figures like the chief assistant.

The situation was obvious!

The strange god didn’t want him to go out anymore.

“Why?”

“Is it deliberately targeting me?”

He didn’t recklessly try to force his way out.

Lin Bei knew what was going on.

Since the strange god had sealed the doors and windows, he definitely couldn’t get out.

“Let’s go back.”

...

A few hours later, the lightning-struck white rose and the lightning-struck red rose woke up one after the other.

Lin Bei decisively asked about the garden.

“What was this garden you mentioned before?”

“Tell me.”

The rose twins, one after the other, told Lin Bei about the garden.

The garden...

A place where various flowers, plants, and trees were planted.

But what was special was that the plants planted in the garden would undergo various mutations.

The two sisters were born because of this.

Before the mutation, they were just two ordinary roses.

And the master of the garden.

was the mysterious god who created the garden.

He was also the first gardener.

Very mysterious, he often planted various plants.

Occasionally, humans would go to the garden and play the important role of gardener.

But to this day, no one has been able to complete the challenge.

All the human gardeners eventually became nutrients for the plants.

Beneath the garden's soil lie countless bones.

...

Ten days passed in the blink of an eye.

The rest period ended.

The cracks on the Phoenix Egg had increased, but it hadn't hatched yet.

The Rose Twins were being struck by lightning more frequently, and now they occasionally experienced electrical leaks when moving around.

The two demons' favorability ratings had both successfully surpassed 800,000.

He had gained several hundred thousand more favorability points.

Lin Bei hadn't spent any of his Terror Coins; he still had over 200 million.

Lin Bei planned to save them up some more.

After saying goodbye, it was exactly midnight.

The familiar voice rang out once more:

[The ten-day rest period has officially ended.]

[A new round of professional challenges will begin in one minute.]

[Please make all necessary preparations and bring what you need.]

[You can only bring things that are entirely yours.]

Lin Bei has a personal space, so he doesn't need to worry about that.

"This job probably won't be easy."

"But it won't be super difficult."

"It'll be revealed soon."

A minute passed in the blink of an eye.

Lin Bei's vision began to blur.

The space shattered, and he lost all five senses.

After a long while, things returned to normal.

Upon opening his eyes, he hurriedly began to observe his surroundings.

The first thing he saw was a red banner.

'Actress Shi Yexiang's new film "To My Beloved" has officially begun filming!'



Chapter 92: Stand-ins and Extras, The Shock of the First Encounter


"A new movie?"

"Actress Shi Yexiang?"

"That name sounds familiar."

"So, is this job related to acting?"

Lin Bei continued to observe the situation.

He was in an open space, without chairs or benches, and even the ground wasn't very smooth.

There were about a hundred challengers, including Lin Bei.

Half were men, half were women.

They were all about the same age.

They were all relatively young.

Not much useful information was being gathered.

However, Lin Bei was fairly certain of one thing.

"This shouldn't be Gray Abyss City."

"I've never heard of any film studios or anything like that in Gray Abyss City."

Just then, the notification sounded.

"Extras and stuntmen."

Lin Bei remembered the movie he'd watched with Yin Yueying.

He remembered the scenes clearly.

It was bloody and terrifying.

Corpses littered the ground, endless slaughter, Shi Yexiang covered in blood, wielding a scythe, dressed in a white dress—she was practically a white grim reaper.

At that moment, Lin Bei only had one feeling:

Realistic!

Utterly realistic!

Whether it was the superb acting or the outstanding special effects, the actors' corpses on the ground were lifeless, the flesh at the cuts by the scythe still writhing.

The blood was real too.

It didn't seem like some kind of movie prop.

He'd been so accommodating to her unreasonable requests all day, what's a small request like this?

He just wanted to rest.

After watching kissing scenes all day.

And those were from an actress renowned for her acting and looks, no less. He was both envious and upset, and had no interest in anything else.

Hoo!

Taking a deep breath, the director glanced at the extras, his gaze subtle, secretly observing them.

He completely ignored the male extras.

"This batch of extras is pretty good."

"I want to take on ten of them!"

In the director's eyes, extras' lives were worthless, and they didn't deserve any self-respect.

They were completely under his control!

It's not just the strange situation where the director, assistant director, casting director, screenwriter, and other important figures hold positions that allows them to do whatever they want with extras.

...

Actress Shi Yexiang was indeed exhausted.

Several hours of non-stop filming would take a toll even on someone made of iron.

But she ignored the director.

Instead, she looked at Lin Bei and said,

"Well, I'll go rest now..."

"You can come find me anytime."

"I'm staying in the best room."

After chatting with Lin Bei for a few more minutes, Actress Shi Yexiang finally left.

Numbers were still appearing above her head.

+1...

+1...

Lin Bei watched Shi Yexiang's retreating figure.

Her related information panel appeared in his field of vision.

【Shi Yexiang (Millennial Female Assassin)】

【Affection Points: 30088】

【Relationship: I love you for billions and billions of years!】

【Level: Demon King】

【

She loves you!

One hundred years isn't enough.

One thousand years isn't enough.

Even ten thousand years wouldn't be enough.

She will always love you.

She will love you for billions and billions of years.

Her love for you is as clear as the sun and moon; she will fulfill your every request, no matter how unreasonable or absurd.

Even if you wanted the male and female leads in a movie to die, she would do it without hesitation.

Because she loves you, she can't help but want to be close to you.

Filming kissing scenes...

is just one way for her to get close to you.

Afterwards, she will find ways to get close to you again.

Although everything she does revolves around you, she also has her own little thoughts.

Every little thought is related to you!

Affected by this aspect, her true form's favorability rating also increased to some extent.

The ratio remained unchanged.

Thirty to one!

When the true form's favorability exceeds one hundred, it will affect all other aspects...

】

Lin Bei's eyebrows twitched slightly.

Unbeknownst to him, the actress Shi Yexiang's favorability points had reached a very high level.

"Thirty thousand favorability points."

"Based on the thirty to one feedback ratio."

"Her true form's favorability has also exceeded one thousand!"

"Not bad."

"Now I've completely won her over."

A smile involuntarily appeared on his lips.

Undoubtedly, the actress Shi Yexiang's status in the entire film crew could even be described as supreme.

From the director and extras to the stunt doubles and extras, everyone had to act according to the actress Shi Yexiang's demands.

And Shi Yexiang?

She revolved around Lin Bei; whatever Lin Bei said went.

“Doesn’t this mean…”

“From now on, this place will be my personal fiefdom?”

“Whoever I want to have more screen time will get more screen time.”

“If I want the male lead to die, then the male lead has to die?”

…

After Shi Yexiang left for a while, the director came to Lin Bei’s side.

“Lin Bei, what’s your relationship with the actress?”

“Have we known each other for a long time?”

“Her behavior today was particularly unusual, completely different from the rumors.”

He was extremely curious.

A human…

receiving such preferential treatment?

It’s surreal.

Extremely unreal!

If nothing was recorded by cameras, no one would believe it, not even a hundred ghosts would tell; everyone would just think it was a rumor.

Lin Bei shook his head.

“I don’t know her.”

“This is the first time I’ve met her.”

He wasn’t lying.

It really was the first time they had met.

As for the real reason, Lin Bei naturally wouldn't say.

"First meeting."

"This happened?"

"I have a feeling the filming won't go too smoothly afterward."

"It's probably just your imagination."

The director didn't press for further questions.

He was just there to meet with Lin Bei.

With the actress Shi Yexiang present, he didn't dare offend Lin Bei.

"Okay, you should go and rest too."

"You've worked very hard today."

"I'll discuss the payment for this scene with you, at the male lead's rate."

"However, let's make it clear beforehand, the price for other scenes will still be based on extras."

Lin Bei didn't waste too much time on the payment issue.

The Terror Coin issue wasn't urgent for now.

Today was only the first day.

He had already realized one thing.

As long as Shi Yexiang was involved, the filming period wouldn't be short.

...

The director and screenwriters quickly left.

The casting director came to Lin Bei's side.

Now, he was completely relieved.

"Brother, I was so worried about you before."

"Hey, you were worrying for nothing."

"With your relationship with Actress Shi Yexiang, who on set would dare to make things difficult for you?"

"I wouldn't need to bother finding you a safe role."

"Maintain your relationship with Actress Shi Yexiang."

"If she makes any unreasonable demands, like asking you to come at night, you'd better agree."

"You know what I mean?"

Lin Bei could easily understand.

But he was a man of principle and had a bottom line; he wouldn't agree to such an excessive request.

"No, I won't agree."

"I've lived an upright life; why would I bow down to her?"

Hearing this, the casting director was very anxious.

He genuinely cared about Lin Bei.

"Brother."

"Take my advice."

"Sometimes."

"Useless dignity doesn't need to be upheld."

"..."

After much persuasion, Lin Bei finally relented.

"Fine."

"If she insists."

"Then I'll reluctantly agree."

Afterward, Lin Bei returned to his room.

Unlike the simple rooms of the other extras, his room was a luxurious suite.
